   282	def classify_tokenized_marker(marker_str, tokens):
   283	    """Determine whether the turn marker tag in marker_str tokenized as ONE special token or several pieces."""
   284	    tag = marker_str[:marker_str.index('>') + 1] if '>' in marker_str else marker_str
   285	    pieces = [t.get('piece') for t in tokens]
   286	    tag_is_single = bool(tokens) and pieces[0] == tag
   287	    whole_is_single = len(tokens) == 1 and tag_is_single
   288	    if whole_is_single:
   289	        summary = 'ONE special token'
   290	    elif tag_is_single:
   291	        summary = f'ONE special token ({tag}) + role token ({len(tokens)} tokens total)'
   292	    else:
   293	        summary = f'several plain-text pieces ({len(tokens)} pieces)'
   294	    return {
   295	        'input': marker_str,
   296	        'tag': tag,
   297	        'token_count': len(tokens),
   298	        'tokens': tokens,
   299	        'pieces': pieces,
   300	        'tag_is_single_special_token': tag_is_single,
   301	        'whole_input_is_single_token': whole_is_single,
   302	        'classification': summary,
   303	    }
   304	
   305	
   306	def evaluate_template_verdict(chat_template, tokenize_results, rendered_hi):
   307	    """Return CONFIRMED / REFUTED / UNCLEAR that Gemma 3 markers are wrong for this model."""
   308	    by_input = {r['input']: r for r in tokenize_results}
   309	    gemma3_plain = (
   310	        not by_input['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   311	        and not by_input['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   312	    )
   313	    gemma4_special = (
   314	        by_input['<|turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   315	        and by_input['<turn|>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   316	        and by_input['<|turn>model']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   317	    )
   318	    template_uses_gemma4 = (
   319	        '<|turn>' in str(chat_template)
   320	        and '<turn|>' in str(chat_template)
   321	        and '<start_of_turn>' not in str(chat_template)
   322	        and '<end_of_turn>' not in str(chat_template)
   323	    )
   324	    rendered_uses_gemma4 = '<|turn>user\nHi<turn|>\n' in str(rendered_hi) and '<|turn>model\n' in str(rendered_hi)
   325	    if gemma3_plain and gemma4_special and template_uses_gemma4 and rendered_uses_gemma4:
   326	        return 'CONFIRMED'
   327	    if (
   328	        by_input['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   329	        and by_input['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   330	    ):
   331	        return 'REFUTED'
   332	    return 'UNCLEAR'
   333	
   334	
   335	def extract_yolo_lines_from_m2q1_record(rec):
   336	    """Extract the Y-ONLY YOLO lines and verify chat.build_prompt reproduces M2-Q1's exact Y-ONLY prompt."""
   337	    old_prompt = rec['gemma_prompts']['Y-ONLY']
   338	    used_dets = rec.get('m2_detections') or rec.get('detections') or []
   339	    yolo_lines = '\n'.join(
   340	        f"- {d['class_name']} [{i}], {d['position']}, {d['distance']}"
   341	        for i, d in enumerate(used_dets)
   342	    )
   343	    user_text = f'{TEXT_INSTRUCTION}\n\n{yolo_lines}'
   344	    rebuilt = build_prompt('gemma', [{'role': 'user', 'content': user_text}])
   345	    if rebuilt != old_prompt:
   346	        raise RuntimeError(f"M2-Q1 Y-ONLY prompt mismatch for {rec.get('photo')}")
   347	    return yolo_lines, user_text, old_prompt
   348	
   349	
   350	def load_target_frames(
   351	    results_json_path=M2Q1_RESULTS_JSON,
   352	    frames_dir=M2Q1_FRAMES_DIR,
   353	    limit=None,
   354	    stems=FRAME_STEMS,
   355	):
   356	    """Load the target frames, enforcing exact frame identity and 640 px JPEG resolution."""
   357	    with open(results_json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
   358	        m2q1 = json.load(f)
   359	    by_stem = {}
   360	    for rec in m2q1.get('photos', []):
   361	        frame_name = Path(rec['frame_file']).name
   362	        stem = frame_name[:-4] if frame_name.endswith('.jpg') else frame_name
   363	        by_stem[stem] = rec
   364	
   365	    selected_stems = stems[:limit] if limit is not None else stems
   366	    items = []
   367	    for wanted in selected_stems:
   368	        if wanted not in by_stem:
   369	            raise RuntimeError(f'Required frame {wanted!r} not found in {results_json_path}')
   370	        matched_stem = wanted
   371	        rec = by_stem[matched_stem]
   372	        frame_path = Path(frames_dir) / f'{matched_stem}.jpg'
   373	        if not frame_path.is_file():
   374	            raise RuntimeError(f'Frame file missing: {frame_path}')
   375	        yolo_lines, user_text, old_prompt = extract_yolo_lines_from_m2q1_record(rec)
   376	        frame_bytes = frame_path.read_bytes()
   377	        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(frame_bytes)) as img_check:
   378	            actual_size = [int(img_check.width), int(img_check.height)]
   379	        if max(actual_size) != 640 or min(actual_size) <= 0:
   380	            raise RuntimeError(
   381	                f'Frame {frame_path} has dimensions {actual_size}, expected 640 px longest edge'
   382	            )
   383	        recorded_size = rec.get('frame_size')
   384	        if recorded_size is not None and [int(recorded_size[0]), int(recorded_size[1])] != actual_size:
   385	            raise RuntimeError(
   386	                f'Frame size mismatch for {matched_stem}: results.json has {recorded_size}, JPEG is {actual_size}'
   387	            )
   388	        frame_sha = hashlib.sha256(frame_bytes).hexdigest()
   389	        items.append({
   390	            'requested_stem': wanted,
   391	            'frame_stem': matched_stem,
   392	            'match_note': 'exact match',
   393	            'photo': rec['photo'],
   394	            'frame_path': str(frame_path),
   395	            'frame_sha256': frame_sha,
   396	            'frame_bytes': len(frame_bytes),
   397	            'frame_size': actual_size,
   398	            'detection_count': rec.get('detection_count'),
   399	            'yolo_lines': yolo_lines,
   400	            'user_text': user_text,
   401	            'old_prompt': old_prompt,
   402	            'm2q1_replies': rec.get('replies'),
   403	        })
   404	    return items
   405	
   406	
   407	def _parse_log_ts_ms(ts_str):
   408	    parts = [int(p) for p in ts_str.split('.')]
   409	    if len(parts) != 4:
   410	        return None
   411	    mins, secs, ms, us = parts
   412	    return ((mins * 60 + secs) * 1000.0) + ms + (us / 1000.0)
   413	
   414	
   415	def parse_server_log_delta(log_delta):
   416	    """Extract logged image token counts and image-encode time (if printed) from a server log slice."""
   417	    batch_tokens = [
   418	        int(m) for m in re.findall(r'decoding image batch \d+/\d+, n_tokens_batch = (\d+)', log_delta)
   419	    ]
   420	    enc_batch_tokens = [
   421	        int(m) for m in re.findall(r'encoding batch with \d+ entries and total (\d+) tokens', log_delta)
   422	    ]
   423	    sampler_tokens = [
   424	        int(tot) - int(txt)
   425	        for txt, tot in re.findall(r'init sampler, took [0-9.]+ ms, tokens: text = (\d+), total = (\d+)', log_delta)
   426	        if int(tot) > int(txt)
   427	    ]
   428	    if batch_tokens:
   429	        image_tokens = sum(batch_tokens)
   430	    elif enc_batch_tokens:
   431	        image_tokens = enc_batch_tokens[-1]
   432	    elif sampler_tokens:
   433	        image_tokens = sampler_tokens[-1]
   434	    else:
   435	        image_tokens = None
   436	
   437	    encode_ms = None
   438	    for pat in (
   439	        r'image_encode[^0-9\n]*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   440	        r'slice\s+encoded\s+in\s+([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   441	        r'encoded\s+in\s+([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   442	        r'encode_mtmd_chunk[^0-9\n]*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   443	    ):
   444	        m = re.search(pat, log_delta, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
   445	        if m:
   446	            encode_ms = float(m.group(1))
   447	            break
   448	
   449	    if encode_ms is None:
   450	        m_enc = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d{2}\.\d{3}\.\d{3})\s+.*encoding mtmd batch from idx', log_delta)
   451	        m_dec = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d{2}\.\d{3}\.\d{3})\s+.*decoding image batch 1/', log_delta)
   452	        if m_enc and m_dec:
   453	            t1 = _parse_log_ts_ms(m_enc.group(1))
   454	            t2 = _parse_log_ts_ms(m_dec.group(1))
   455	            if t1 is not None and t2 is not None and t2 >= t1:
   456	                encode_ms = round(t2 - t1, 3)
   457	
   458	    return {
   459	        'image_tokens_logged': image_tokens,
   460	        'image_tokens_batches': batch_tokens,
   461	        'image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement': encode_ms,
   462	    }
   463	
   464	
   465	def read_vmhwm_kib_for_tree(timeout_pid):
   466	    """Read VmHWM (in KiB) from /proc/<pid>/status for timeout_pid and any direct child (llama-server)."""
   467	    best_kib = None
   468	    best_pid = None
   469	    best_name = None
   470	    for status_path in glob.glob('/proc/[0-9]*/status'):
   471	        try:
   472	            pid = int(status_path.split('/')[2])
   473	            text = Path(status_path).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
   474	        except OSError:
   475	            continue
   476	        ppid = None
   477	        name = None
   478	        hwm_kib = None
   479	        for line in text.splitlines():
   480	            if line.startswith('Name:'):
   481	                name = line.split(':', 1)[1].strip()
   482	            elif line.startswith('PPid:'):
   483	                try:
   484	                    ppid = int(line.split(':', 1)[1].strip())
   485	                except ValueError:
   486	                    pass
   487	            elif line.startswith('VmHWM:'):
   488	                parts = line.split()
   489	                if len(parts) >= 2 and parts[1].isdigit():
   490	                    hwm_kib = int(parts[1])
   491	        if pid == timeout_pid or ppid == timeout_pid:
   492	            if hwm_kib is not None and (best_kib is None or hwm_kib > best_kib):
   493	                best_kib = hwm_kib
   494	                best_pid = pid
   495	                best_name = name
   496	    return {'pid': best_pid, 'name': best_name, 'vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': best_kib}
   497	
   498	
   499	def check_port_8080_stopped(port=ROBOT_PORT):
   500	    """Ensure the robot server on port 8080 is stopped before starting a test server on 8090."""
   501	    try:
   502	        with urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health', timeout=2) as resp:
   503	            body = resp.read().decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
   504	            raise RuntimeError(
   505	                f'STOP: robot server on 127.0.0.1:{port} is still running ({resp.status}: {body}). '
   506	                'Owner must stop it before running VIS-0.'
   507	            )
   508	    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
   509	        body = ''
   510	        if e.fp is not None:
   511	            try:
   512	                body = e.fp.read().decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
   513	            except Exception:
   514	                body = ''
   515	        raise RuntimeError(
   516	            f'STOP: robot server on 127.0.0.1:{port} responded with HTTP {e.code} ({e.reason}: {body}). '
   517	            'Owner must stop it before running VIS-0.'
   518	        ) from e
   519	    except urllib.error.URLError as e:
   520	        reason = getattr(e, 'reason', e)
   521	        if not isinstance(reason, ConnectionRefusedError):
   522	            raise RuntimeError(
   523	                f'STOP: could not confirm 127.0.0.1:{port} is stopped (unexpected URLError: {e}). '
   524	                'Owner must ensure port 8080 is down before running VIS-0.'
   525	            ) from e
   526	    check_port_free(port)
   527	    return True
   528	
   529	
   530	def check_port_free(port=TEST_PORT):
   531	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
   532	        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
   533	        try:
   534	            sock.bind((TEST_HOST, port))
   535	        except OSError as e:
   536	            raise RuntimeError(f'Port {TEST_HOST}:{port} is already in use: {e}') from e
   537	
   538	
   539	def http_json(path, body=None, base_url=TEST_URL, timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT_S):
   540	    data = None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode('utf-8')
   541	    req = urllib.request.Request(
   542	        base_url + path,
   543	        data=data,
   544	        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   545	        method='GET' if data is None else 'POST',
   546	    )
   547	    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
   548	        return json.loads(resp.read().decode('utf-8'))
   549	
   550	
   551	def extract_chat_reply(resp_json):
   552	    """Extract assistant reply content, reasoning_content, finish_reason, usage, and timings from /chat/completions."""
   553	    choices = resp_json.get('choices') or []
   554	    if not choices:
   555	        raise RuntimeError(f'No choices in /chat/completions response: {resp_json}')
   556	    choice0 = choices[0]
   557	    msg = choice0.get('message') or {}
   558	    raw_content = msg.get('content')
   559	    reasoning_content = msg.get('reasoning_content')
   560	    content = '' if raw_content is None else str(raw_content).strip()
   561	    finish_reason = choice0.get('finish_reason')
   562	    usage = resp_json.get('usage') or {}
   563	    timings = resp_json.get('timings') or {}
   564	    prompt_tokens = usage.get('prompt_tokens')
   565	    if prompt_tokens is None:
   566	        prompt_tokens = timings.get('prompt_n')
   567	    completion_tokens = usage.get('completion_tokens')
   568	    if completion_tokens is None:
   569	        completion_tokens = timings.get('predicted_n')
   570	    return {
   571	        'reply': content,
   572	        'raw_content': raw_content,
   573	        'reasoning_content': reasoning_content,
   574	        'finish_reason': finish_reason,
   575	        'prompt_tokens': prompt_tokens,
   576	        'completion_tokens': completion_tokens,
   577	        'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('prompt_ms'),
   578	        'predicted_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_ms'),
   579	        'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_per_second'),
   580	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   581	    }
   582	
   583	
   584	def extract_completion_reply(resp_json):
   585	    """Extract reply content and timings from /completion."""
   586	    if 'content' not in resp_json:
   587	        raise RuntimeError(f'Unexpected /completion response: {resp_json}')
   588	    raw_content = str(resp_json['content'])
   589	    timings = resp_json.get('timings') or {}
   590	    return {
   591	        'reply': raw_content.strip(),
   592	        'raw_content': raw_content,
   593	        'stop_type': resp_json.get('stop_type'),
   594	        'stopping_word': resp_json.get('stopping_word'),
   595	        'tokens_evaluated': resp_json.get('tokens_evaluated', timings.get('prompt_n')),
   596	        'tokens_predicted': resp_json.get('tokens_predicted', timings.get('predicted_n')),
   597	        'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('prompt_ms'),
   598	        'predicted_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_ms'),
   599	        'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_per_second'),
   600	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   601	    }
   602	
   603	
   604	def format_server_log_banner(started_utc, label, budget, cmd):
   605	    return (
   606	        f"\n{'=' * 72}\n"
   607	        f"[{started_utc}] START {label} (budget={budget})\n"
   608	        f"LABELS: {REPORT_LABELS}\n"
   609	        f"TIMINGS/MEMORY: {TIMING_LABEL}\n"
   610	        f"CMD: {' '.join(cmd)}\n"
   611	        f"{'=' * 72}\n"
   612	    )
   613	
   614	
   615	class ManagedTestServer:
   616	    """Manages a single timeout-wrapped llama-server instance on port 8090."""
   617	
   618	    def __init__(self, budget, server_log_path, label=''):
   619	        self.budget = budget
   620	        self.server_log_path = Path(server_log_path)
   621	        self.label = label or f'budget_{budget}'
   622	        self.cmd = build_server_cmd(budget=budget)
   623	        self.proc = None
   624	        self.log_handle = None
   625	        self.peak_vmhwm_kib = None
   626	        self.server_pid = None
   627	        self.server_name = None
   628	        self.started_utc = None
   629	        self.ended_utc = None
   630	        self.startup_s_not_a_measurement = None
   631	        self.exit_code = None
   632	
   633	    def sample_vmhwm(self):
   634	        if self.proc is None:
   635	            return None
   636	        info = read_vmhwm_kib_for_tree(self.proc.pid)
   637	        hwm = info['vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement']
   638	        if hwm is not None and (self.peak_vmhwm_kib is None or hwm > self.peak_vmhwm_kib):
   639	            self.peak_vmhwm_kib = hwm
   640	            self.server_pid = info['pid']
   641	            self.server_name = info['name']
   642	        return self.peak_vmhwm_kib
   643	
   644	    def start(self):
   645	        check_port_8080_stopped(ROBOT_PORT)
   646	        check_port_free(TEST_PORT)
   647	        self.server_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   648	        self.started_utc = utc_now()
   649	        banner = format_server_log_banner(self.started_utc, self.label, self.budget, self.cmd)
   650	        with open(self.server_log_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as lf:
   651	            lf.write(banner)
   652	        self.log_handle = open(self.server_log_path, 'ab')
   653	        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith('LLAMA_ARG_')}
   654	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   655	        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(
   656	            self.cmd,
   657	            env=env,
   658	            stdout=self.log_handle,
   659	            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   660	        )
   661	        deadline = time.perf_counter() + STARTUP_TIMEOUT_S
   662	        while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
   663	            if self.proc.poll() is not None:
   664	                self.exit_code = self.proc.returncode
   665	                raise RuntimeError(
   666	                    f'llama-server exited during startup with code {self.exit_code}; '
   667	                    f'tail:\n{self.log_tail(25)}'
   668	                )
   669	            try:
   670	                health = http_json('/health', timeout=2)
   671	                if health.get('status') == 'ok':
   672	                    self.startup_s_not_a_measurement = time.perf_counter() - t0
   673	                    self.sample_vmhwm()
   674	                    return
   675	            except Exception:
   676	                pass
   677	            time.sleep(0.5)
   678	        self.stop()
   679	        raise TimeoutError(f'llama-server did not become healthy within {STARTUP_TIMEOUT_S}s')
   680	
   681	    def is_alive(self):
   682	        return self.proc is not None and self.proc.poll() is None
   683	
   684	    def log_size(self):
   685	        try:
   686	            if self.log_handle:
   687	                self.log_handle.flush()
   688	            return self.server_log_path.stat().st_size
   689	        except OSError:
   690	            return 0
   691	
   692	    def log_delta_from(self, offset):
   693	        try:
   694	            if self.log_handle:
   695	                self.log_handle.flush()
   696	            with open(self.server_log_path, 'rb') as f:
   697	                f.seek(offset)
   698	                return f.read().decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
   699	        except OSError:
   700	            return ''
   701	
   702	    def log_tail(self, n_lines=25):
   703	        try:
   704	            if self.log_handle:
   705	                self.log_handle.flush()
   706	            lines = self.server_log_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').splitlines()
   707	            return '\n'.join(lines[-n_lines:])
   708	        except OSError:
   709	            return ''
   710	
   711	    def stop(self):
   712	        self.sample_vmhwm()
   713	        if self.proc is not None:
   714	            if self.proc.poll() is None:
   715	                self.proc.terminate()
   716	                try:
   717	                    self.proc.wait(timeout=10)
   718	                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   719	                    self.proc.kill()
   720	                    self.proc.wait(timeout=10)
   721	            self.exit_code = self.proc.returncode
   722	        if self.log_handle is not None:
   723	            try:
   724	                self.log_handle.flush()
   725	                self.log_handle.close()
   726	            except OSError:
   727	                pass
   728	            self.log_handle = None
   729	        self.ended_utc = utc_now()
   730	
   731	    def summary(self):
   732	        return {
   733	            'label': self.label,
   734	            'budget': self.budget,
   735	            'command': self.cmd,
   736	            'started_utc': self.started_utc,
   737	            'ended_utc': self.ended_utc,
   738	            'startup_s_not_a_measurement': self.startup_s_not_a_measurement,
   739	            'server_pid': self.server_pid,
   740	            'server_name': self.server_name,
   741	            'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': self.peak_vmhwm_kib,
   742	            'exit_code': self.exit_code,
   743	            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   744	        }
   745	
   746	
   747	def run_step_a_template_check(target_frames, post_fn=http_json, get_fn=http_json, sample_hwm_fn=None):
   748	    """Run Step A (template check + text A/B on the target frames) against the server on 8090."""
   749	    props = get_fn('/props')
   750	    chat_template = props.get('chat_template', '')
   751	    dgs = props.get('default_generation_settings') or {}
   752	    props_summary = {
   753	        'model_alias': props.get('model_alias'),
   754	        'model_path': props.get('model_path'),
   755	        'model_ftype': props.get('model_ftype'),
   756	        'total_slots': props.get('total_slots'),
   757	        'n_ctx': dgs.get('n_ctx'),
   758	        'build_info': props.get('build_info'),
   759	        'bos_token': props.get('bos_token'),
   760	        'eos_token': props.get('eos_token'),
   761	        'modalities': props.get('modalities'),
   762	    }
   763	
   764	    tokenize_results = []
   765	    for marker in TOKENIZE_MARKERS:
   766	        res = post_fn('/tokenize', build_tokenize_payload(marker))
   767	        tokens = res.get('tokens') or []
   768	        tokenize_results.append(classify_tokenized_marker(marker, tokens))
   769	
   770	    apply_res = post_fn('/apply-template', build_apply_template_payload(APPLY_TEMPLATE_MESSAGES))
   771	    rendered_hi = apply_res.get('prompt', '')
   772	
   773	    verdict = evaluate_template_verdict(chat_template, tokenize_results, rendered_hi)
   774	
   775	    text_ab = []
   776	    for item in target_frames:
   777	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   778	        old_raw = post_fn('/completion', build_old_completion_payload(item['old_prompt']))
   779	        old_wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
   780	        old_parsed = extract_completion_reply(old_raw)
   781	        old_parsed['wall_ms_not_a_measurement'] = old_wall_ms
   782	        if sample_hwm_fn:
   783	            sample_hwm_fn()
   784	
   785	        t1 = time.perf_counter()
   786	        new_raw = post_fn('/chat/completions', build_new_chat_text_payload(item['user_text']))
   787	        new_wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t1) * 1000.0
   788	        new_parsed = extract_chat_reply(new_raw)
   789	        new_parsed['wall_ms_not_a_measurement'] = new_wall_ms
   790	        if sample_hwm_fn:
   791	            sample_hwm_fn()
   792	
   793	        text_ab.append({
   794	            'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
   795	            'photo': item['photo'],
   796	            'yolo_lines': item['yolo_lines'],
   797	            'old_completion': old_parsed,
   798	            'new_chat_completions': new_parsed,
   799	            'm2q1_saved_replies': item.get('m2q1_replies'),
   800	            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   801	        })
   802	
   803	    return {
   804	        'verdict': verdict,
   805	        'verdict_statement': (
   806	            f'{verdict}: Gemma 3 markers (<start_of_turn>, <end_of_turn>) are tokenized as multiple '
   807	            'plain-text pieces, whereas Gemma 4 markers (<|turn>, <turn|>) are single special tokens '
   808	            'and are used by the GGUF chat_template.'
   809	            if verdict == 'CONFIRMED'
   810	            else f'{verdict}: see tokenize_checks and rendered_hi.'
   811	        ),
   812	        'props_summary': props_summary,
   813	        'chat_template': chat_template,
   814	        'tokenize_checks': tokenize_results,
   815	        'apply_template_hi': {
   816	            'messages': APPLY_TEMPLATE_MESSAGES,
   817	            'rendered_prompt': rendered_hi,
   818	        },
   819	        'text_ab': text_ab,
   820	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   821	    }
   822	
   823	
   824	def run_vision_budget_pass(
   825	    budget,
   826	    target_frames,
   827	    server_factory,
   828	    post_fn=http_json,
   829	    with_yolo=False,
   830	    log_fn=print,
   831	):
   832	    """Run one vision budget pass across target_frames with crash handling (restart at most once, stop on 2 consecutive crashes)."""
   833	    label = f'vision_budget_{budget}_yolo' if with_yolo else f'vision_budget_{budget}'
   834	    server = server_factory(budget=budget, label=label)
   835	    server_summaries = []
   836	    calls = []
   837	    crashes = 0
   838	    consecutive_crashes = 0
   839	    restarted_once = False
   840	    stopped_early = False
   841	    stop_reason = None
   842	
   843	    try:
   844	        server.start()
   845	    except Exception as e:
   846	        server.stop()
   847	        server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   848	        return {
   849	            'budget': budget,
   850	            'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   851	            'optional': bool(with_yolo),
   852	            'crashes': 1,
   853	            'stopped_early': True,
   854	            'stop_reason': f'startup_failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}',
   855	            'servers': server_summaries,
   856	            'calls': [],
   857	            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   858	        }
   859	
   860	    try:
   861	        for idx, item in enumerate(target_frames):
   862	            img_bytes = Path(item['frame_path']).read_bytes()
   863	            prompt_text = (
   864	                build_optional_yolo_vision_text(item['yolo_lines'])
   865	                if with_yolo
   866	                else VISION_PROMPT
   867	            )
   868	            payload = build_vision_chat_payload(img_bytes, text_prompt=prompt_text)
   869	            offset = server.log_size()
   870	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
   871	            try:
   872	                resp = post_fn('/chat/completions', payload)
   873	                wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
   874	                server.sample_vmhwm()
   875	                delta = server.log_delta_from(offset)
   876	                log_info = parse_server_log_delta(delta)
   877	                parsed = extract_chat_reply(resp)
   878	                call_rec = {
   879	                    'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
   880	                    'photo': item['photo'],
   881	                    'budget': budget,
   882	                    'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   883	                    'status': 'ok',
   884	                    'crashed': False,
   885	                    'reply': parsed['reply'],
   886	                    'raw_content': parsed['raw_content'],
   887	                    'reasoning_content': parsed['reasoning_content'],
   888	                    'finish_reason': parsed['finish_reason'],
   889	                    'prompt_tokens': parsed['prompt_tokens'],
   890	                    'completion_tokens': parsed['completion_tokens'],
   891	                    'image_tokens_logged': log_info['image_tokens_logged'],
   892	                    'image_tokens_batches': log_info['image_tokens_batches'],
   893	                    'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': parsed['prompt_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   894	                    'predicted_ms_not_a_measurement': parsed['predicted_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   895	                    'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': parsed['predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement'],
   896	                    'wall_ms_not_a_measurement': wall_ms,
   897	                    'image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement': log_info['image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   898	                    'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   899	                }
   900	                calls.append(call_rec)
   901	                consecutive_crashes = 0
   902	                log_fn(
   903	                    f"[{label}] {item['frame_stem']}: ok, img_tok={log_info['image_tokens_logged']}, "
   904	                    f"prompt_tok={parsed['prompt_tokens']}, wall_ms={wall_ms:.1f} ({TIMING_LABEL})"
   905	                )
   906	            except Exception as e:
   907	                wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
   908	                time.sleep(0.2)
   909	                alive = server.is_alive()
   910	                delta = server.log_delta_from(offset)
   911	                log_info = parse_server_log_delta(delta)
   912	                tail = server.log_tail(30)
   913	                is_http_error = isinstance(e, urllib.error.HTTPError)
   914	                is_crash = (not alive) or (
   915	                    not is_http_error
   916	                    and isinstance(e, (http.client.RemoteDisconnected, ConnectionResetError, urllib.error.URLError))
   917	                )
   918	                if is_crash:
   919	                    crashes += 1
   920	                    consecutive_crashes += 1
   921	                call_rec = {
   922	                    'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
   923	                    'photo': item['photo'],
   924	                    'budget': budget,
   925	                    'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   926	                    'status': 'crash' if is_crash else 'error',
   927	                    'crashed': is_crash,
   928	                    'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}',
   929	                    'server_exit_code': server.proc.poll() if server.proc is not None else None,
   930	                    'reply': None,
   931	                    'prompt_tokens': None,
   932	                    'image_tokens_logged': log_info['image_tokens_logged'],
   933	                    'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': None,
   934	                    'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': None,
   935	                    'wall_ms_not_a_measurement': wall_ms,
   936	                    'image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement': log_info['image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   937	                    'log_tail': tail,
   938	                    'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   939	                }
   940	                calls.append(call_rec)
   941	                log_fn(f"[{label}] {item['frame_stem']}: {'CRASH' if is_crash else 'ERROR'} ({e})")
   942	
   943	                if is_crash:
   944	                    server.stop()
   945	                    server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   946	                    if consecutive_crashes >= 2:
   947	                        stopped_early = True
   948	                        stop_reason = 'two_consecutive_crashes_at_budget'
   949	                        break
   950	                    if restarted_once:
   951	                        stopped_early = True
   952	                        stop_reason = 'already_restarted_once_at_budget'
   953	                        break
   954	                    if idx + 1 < len(target_frames):
   955	                        restarted_once = True
   956	                        server = server_factory(budget=budget, label=f'{label}_restart1')
   957	                        try:
   958	                            server.start()
   959	                        except Exception as re_err:
   960	                            crashes += 1
   961	                            consecutive_crashes += 1
   962	                            server.stop()
   963	                            server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   964	                            stopped_early = True
   965	                            stop_reason = f'restart_failed: {type(re_err).__name__}: {re_err}'
   966	                            break
   967	    finally:
   968	        if server.proc is not None and (not server_summaries or server_summaries[-1]['started_utc'] != server.started_utc):
   969	            server.stop()
   970	            server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   971	
   972	    return {
   973	        'budget': budget,
   974	        'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   975	        'optional': bool(with_yolo),
   976	        'crashes': crashes,
   977	        'stopped_early': stopped_early,
   978	        'stop_reason': stop_reason,
   979	        'servers': server_summaries,
   980	        'calls': calls,
   981	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   982	    }
   983	
   984	
   985	def _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, x, y, lines, width_chars=100, font_size=13, line_gap=4, fill='#111111'):
   986	    font = ImageFont.load_default(size=font_size)
   987	    for raw_line in lines:
   988	        text = str(raw_line)
   989	        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(text, width=width_chars) if text else ['']
   990	        for part in wrapped:
   991	            draw.text((x, y), part, fill=fill, font=font)
   992	            y += font_size + line_gap
   993	    return y
   994	
   995	
   996	def build_vis0_sheet_pdf(results_data, target_frames, pdf_path):
   997	    """Build vis0_sheet.pdf (PIL only, < 20 MB): Page 1 = template check; then 1 page per frame."""
   998	    page_w, page_h = 960, 1480
   999	    total_pages = 1 + len(target_frames)
  1000	    pages = []
  1001	
  1002	    # Page 1: Template Check Results
  1003	    p1 = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
  1004	    draw1 = ImageDraw.Draw(p1)
  1005	    draw1.text(
  1006	        (24, 18),
  1007	        f'VIS-0 Template Check & Vision Feasibility  --  Page 1 / {total_pages}',
  1008	        fill='#000000',
  1009	        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18),
  1010	    )
  1011	    y = 48
  1012	    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(
  1013	        draw1, 24, y, [f'Labels: {REPORT_LABELS}'], width_chars=106, font_size=12, fill='#333333'
  1014	    )
  1015	    y += 10
  1016	
  1017	    tc = results_data['template_check']
  1018	    draw1.text(
  1019	        (24, y),
  1020	        f"Step A Verdict: {tc['verdict']}",
  1021	        fill='#000000',
  1022	        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=16),
  1023	    )
  1024	    y += 24
  1025	    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, [tc['verdict_statement']], width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1026	    y += 12
  1027	
  1028	    draw1.text(
  1029	        (24, y),
  1030	        '1. /tokenize with "with_pieces": true:',
  1031	        fill='#000000',
  1032	        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1033	    )
  1034	    y += 22
  1035	    tok_lines = []
  1036	    for t_row in tc['tokenize_checks']:
  1037	        pieces_str = ', '.join(
  1038	            f"{t['id']}:{t['piece']!r}" for t in t_row['tokens']
  1039	        )
  1040	        tok_lines.append(
  1041	            f"- {t_row['input']!r} -> {t_row['classification']}  [{pieces_str}]"
  1042	        )
  1043	    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, tok_lines, width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1044	    y += 12
  1045	
  1046	    draw1.text(
  1047	        (24, y),
  1048	        '2. /apply-template with [{"role": "user", "content": "Hi"}] (verbatim repr):',
  1049	        fill='#000000',
  1050	        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1051	    )
  1052	    y += 22
  1053	    rendered_hi = tc['apply_template_hi']['rendered_prompt']
  1054	    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, [repr(rendered_hi)], width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1055	    y += 12
  1056	
  1057	    draw1.text(
  1058	        (24, y),
  1059	        '3. /props chat_template header & turn-token lines:',
  1060	        fill='#000000',
  1061	        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1062	    )
  1063	    y += 22
  1064	    ct_lines = tc['chat_template'].splitlines()
  1065	    ct_excerpt = ct_lines[:6] + ['...'] + [
  1066	        ln.strip() for ln in ct_lines if '<|turn>' in ln or '<turn|>' in ln
  1067	    ][:8]
  1068	    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, ct_excerpt, width_chars=102, font_size=12, fill='#222222')
  1069	    y += 12
  1070	
  1071	    draw1.text(
  1072	        (24, y),
  1073	        '4. Vision Budgets Summary (NOT measurements; agent resident):',
  1074	        fill='#000000',
  1075	        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1076	    )
  1077	    y += 22
  1078	    vis_summary_lines = []
  1079	    for b_key, b_pass in results_data.get('vision_budgets', {}).items():
  1080	        ok_calls = [c for c in b_pass['calls'] if c['status'] == 'ok']
  1081	        img_toks = sorted({c['image_tokens_logged'] for c in ok_calls if c.get('image_tokens_logged') is not None})
  1082	        vis_summary_lines.append(
  1083	            f"- Budget {b_key}: {len(ok_calls)}/{len(b_pass['calls'])} ok, "
  1084	            f"crashes={b_pass['crashes']}, logged_image_tokens={img_toks}"
  1085	        )
  1086	    opt_pass = results_data.get('optional_140_yolo')
  1087	    if opt_pass:
  1088	        ok_opt = [c for c in opt_pass['calls'] if c['status'] == 'ok']
  1089	        vis_summary_lines.append(
  1090	            f"- Optional 140+YOLO: {len(ok_opt)}/{len(opt_pass['calls'])} ok, crashes={opt_pass['crashes']}"
  1091	        )
  1092	    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, vis_summary_lines, width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1093	    pages.append(p1)
  1094	
  1095	    # Index lookup for per-frame pages
  1096	    text_by_stem = {r['frame_stem']: r for r in tc['text_ab']}
  1097	    vis_by_budget_stem = {}
  1098	    for b_key, b_pass in results_data.get('vision_budgets', {}).items():
  1099	        vis_by_budget_stem[str(b_key)] = {c['frame_stem']: c for c in b_pass['calls']}
  1100	    opt_by_stem = {}
  1101	    if opt_pass:
  1102	        opt_by_stem = {c['frame_stem']: c for c in opt_pass['calls']}
  1103	
  1104	    for idx, item in enumerate(target_frames, 2):
  1105	        stem = item['frame_stem']
  1106	        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
  1107	        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
  1108	        draw.text(
  1109	            (24, 16),
  1110	            f"{stem} ({item['photo']})  --  Page {idx} / {total_pages}",
  1111	            fill='#000000',
  1112	            font=ImageFont.load_default(size=17),
  1113	        )
  1114	
  1115	        with Image.open(item['frame_path']) as raw_img:
  1116	            disp_img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(raw_img).convert('RGB')
  1117	        disp_img.thumbnail((560, 420), Image.Resampling.BILINEAR)
  1118	        img_x = (page_w - disp_img.width) // 2
  1119	        img_y = 44
  1120	        page.paste(disp_img, (img_x, img_y))
  1121	        y = img_y + disp_img.height + 12
  1122	
  1123	        draw.text((24, y), 'YOLO detections (Y-ONLY input lines):', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=14))
  1124	        y += 18
  1125	        yolo_preview = item['yolo_lines'].splitlines()
  1126	        if len(yolo_preview) > 6:
  1127	            yolo_preview = ['; '.join(yolo_preview[:6]), '; '.join(yolo_preview[6:])]
  1128	        else:
  1129	            yolo_preview = ['; '.join(yolo_preview)] if yolo_preview else ['(none)']

  1140	            fill='#8b0000',

  1159	        if not new_reply_text and new_c.get('reasoning_content'):
  1160	            new_reply_text = f"[empty content; reasoning_content]: {new_c['reasoning_content']}"
  1161	        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, new_reply_text.splitlines() or ['(empty reply)'], width_chars=104, font_size=13)
  1162	        y += 10
  1163	
  1164	        for b in VISION_BUDGETS:

  1167	            if v_call is None:

  1169	                body_lines = ['(not run)']
  1170	            elif v_call['status'] != 'ok':
  1171	                hdr = f"Vision budget {b}: {v_call['status'].upper()} ({v_call.get('error', '')})"
  1172	                body_lines = (v_call.get('log_tail') or '').splitlines()[-3:] or ['(no output)']
  1173	            else:
  1174	                hdr = (
  1175	                    f"Vision budget {b} [prompt_tokens={v_call.get('prompt_tokens')}, "
  1176	                    f"image_tokens_logged={v_call.get('image_tokens_logged')}]:"
  1177	                )
  1178	                v_txt = v_call.get('reply') or ''
  1179	                if not v_txt and v_call.get('reasoning_content'):
  1180	                    v_txt = f"[empty content; reasoning_content]: {v_call['reasoning_content']}"
  1181	                body_lines = v_txt.splitlines() or ['(empty reply)']
  1182	            draw.text((24, y), hdr, fill='#003366', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=14))
  1183	            y += 18
  1184	            y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, body_lines, width_chars=104, font_size=13)
  1185	            y += 8
  1186	
  1187	        if stem in opt_by_stem:
  1188	            o_call = opt_by_stem[stem]
  1189	            hdr = (
  1190	                f"Optional Vision 140 + YOLO [prompt_tokens={o_call.get('prompt_tokens')}, "

  1209	def git_base_commit(root=ROBOT):
  1210	    return subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip()
  1211	
  1212	
  1213	RUN_OUTPUT_FILES = ('results.json', 'manifest.json', 'server.log', 'run.log', 'vis0_sheet.pdf')
  1214	
  1215	
  1216	def resolve_and_validate_out_dir(out_dir=None, runs_root=None):
  1217	    """Resolve out_dir under benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/runs/ and reject outside paths or existing outputs."""
  1218	    allowed_root = Path(HERE / 'runs' if runs_root is None else runs_root).resolve()
  1219	    if out_dir is None:
  1220	        stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
  1221	        resolved = (allowed_root / stamp).resolve()
  1222	    else:
  1223	        resolved = Path(out_dir).resolve()
  1224	    if resolved == allowed_root or not resolved.is_relative_to(allowed_root):
  1225	        raise ValueError(
  1226	            f'--out must resolve to a subdirectory inside {allowed_root}, got {resolved}'
  1227	        )
  1228	    for fname in RUN_OUTPUT_FILES:
  1229	        existing = resolved / fname
  1230	        if existing.exists():
  1231	            raise FileExistsError(f'Refusing to overwrite existing output artifact: {existing}')
  1232	    return resolved

  1234	
  1235	def run_harness(limit=None, out_dir=None, skip_optional=False):
  1236	    start_utc = utc_now()
  1237	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
  1238	    out_dir = resolve_and_validate_out_dir(out_dir=out_dir)
  1239	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1240	    run_log_path = out_dir / 'run.log'
  1241	    server_log_path = out_dir / 'server.log'
  1242	
  1243	    def log(msg):
  1244	        line = f'[{utc_now()}] {msg}'
  1245	        print(line, flush=True)
  1246	        with open(run_log_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as lf:
  1247	            lf.write(line + '\n')
  1248	
  1249	    log(f'START VIS-0 harness ({REPORT_LABELS})')
  1250	    check_port_8080_stopped(ROBOT_PORT)
  1251	
  1252	    for req_file, exp_bytes, exp_sha in (
  1253	        (MODEL_GGUF, MODEL_EXPECTED_BYTES, MODEL_EXPECTED_SHA256),
  1254	        (MMPROJ_GGUF, MMPROJ_EXPECTED_BYTES, MMPROJ_EXPECTED_SHA256),
  1255	    ):
  1256	        if not req_file.is_file():
  1257	            raise RuntimeError(f'Missing required weight file: {req_file}')
  1258	        actual_bytes = req_file.stat().st_size
  1259	        actual_sha = sha256_file(req_file)
  1260	        if actual_bytes != exp_bytes or actual_sha != exp_sha:
  1261	            raise RuntimeError(
  1262	                f'Weight verification failed for {req_file}: '
  1263	                f'bytes={actual_bytes} (expected {exp_bytes}), sha256={actual_sha} (expected {exp_sha})'
  1264	            )
  1265	
  1266	    target_frames = load_target_frames(limit=limit)
  1267	    log(f'Loaded {len(target_frames)} target frames from {M2Q1_RUN_DIR} (limit={limit})')
  1268	
  1269	    all_server_summaries = []
  1270	
  1271	    # Step A runs on the first server instance (started with budget 70), then we run Step B budget 70 on it,
  1272	    # or we run Step A on a dedicated budget=70 server and then run budgets 70, 140, 280 cleanly.
  1273	    # Starting one server for Step A (with --image-min-tokens 70 --image-max-tokens 70) and stopping it cleanly
  1274	    # before the Step B budget loop keeps Step A and each Step B budget completely isolated.
  1275	    step_a_server = ManagedTestServer(budget=VISION_BUDGETS[0], server_log_path=server_log_path, label='step_a_template_check')
  1276	    try:
  1277	        step_a_server.start()
  1278	        log(f'Step A server ready on {TEST_URL} (pid={step_a_server.server_pid})')
  1279	        template_check = run_step_a_template_check(
  1280	            target_frames,
  1281	            post_fn=http_json,
  1282	            get_fn=http_json,
  1283	            sample_hwm_fn=step_a_server.sample_vmhwm,
  1284	        )
  1285	        log(f"Step A complete: verdict={template_check['verdict']}")
  1286	    finally:
  1287	        step_a_server.stop()
  1288	        all_server_summaries.append(step_a_server.summary())
  1289	
  1290	    def server_factory(budget, label):
  1291	        return ManagedTestServer(budget=budget, server_log_path=server_log_path, label=label)
  1292	
  1293	    vision_budgets = {}
  1294	    for budget in VISION_BUDGETS:
  1295	        log(f'Starting Step B vision pass for budget={budget}')
  1296	        b_res = run_vision_budget_pass(
  1297	            budget=budget,
  1298	            target_frames=target_frames,
  1299	            server_factory=server_factory,
  1300	            post_fn=http_json,
  1301	            with_yolo=False,
  1302	            log_fn=log,
  1303	        )
  1304	        vision_budgets[str(budget)] = b_res
  1305	        all_server_summaries.extend(b_res['servers'])
  1306	
  1307	    all_budgets_ok = all(
  1308	        b_res['crashes'] == 0
  1309	        and not b_res['stopped_early']
  1310	        and len(b_res['calls']) == len(target_frames)
  1311	        and all(c['status'] == 'ok' for c in b_res['calls'])
  1312	        for b_res in vision_budgets.values()
  1313	    )
  1314	
  1315	    optional_140_yolo = None
  1316	    if all_budgets_ok and not skip_optional:
  1317	        log('All budgets (70, 140, 280) succeeded without crash; running optional 140 + YOLO pass')
  1318	        optional_140_yolo = run_vision_budget_pass(
  1319	            budget=140,
  1320	            target_frames=target_frames,
  1321	            server_factory=server_factory,
  1322	            post_fn=http_json,
  1323	            with_yolo=True,
  1324	            log_fn=log,
  1325	        )
  1326	        all_server_summaries.extend(optional_140_yolo['servers'])
  1327	
  1328	    peak_vmhwm_kib = max(
  1329	        (s['peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement'] for s in all_server_summaries if s['peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement'] is not None),
  1330	        default=None,
  1331	    )
  1332	
  1333	    results_payload = {
  1334	        'task': 'VIS-0',
  1335	        'labels': REPORT_LABELS,
  1336	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
  1337	        'limit': limit,
  1338	        'template_check': template_check,
  1339	        'vision_budgets': vision_budgets,
  1340	        'optional_140_yolo': optional_140_yolo,
  1341	        'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': peak_vmhwm_kib,
  1342	        'servers': all_server_summaries,
  1343	    }
  1344	    write_json(out_dir / 'results.json', results_payload)
  1345	
  1346	    pdf_path = out_dir / 'vis0_sheet.pdf'
  1347	    build_vis0_sheet_pdf(results_payload, target_frames, pdf_path)
  1348	    DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1349	    shutil.copy2(pdf_path, DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR / 'vis0_sheet.pdf')
  1350	    shutil.copy2(pdf_path, DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR / f'vis0_sheet_{out_dir.name}.pdf')
  1351	    log(f'Built {pdf_path.name} ({pdf_path.stat().st_size} B) and copied to {DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR}')
  1352	
  1353	    end_utc = utc_now()
  1354	    elapsed_s = time.perf_counter() - t0
  1355	    code_hashes = verify_and_hash_imports(ROBOT)
  1356	
  1357	    manifest = {
  1358	        'task': 'VIS-0',
  1359	        'labels': REPORT_LABELS,
  1360	        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
  1361	        'base_commit': git_base_commit(ROBOT),
  1362	        'start_utc': start_utc,
  1363	        'end_utc': end_utc,
  1364	        'elapsed_s_not_a_measurement': elapsed_s,
  1365	        'limit': limit,
  1366	        'code_sha256': code_hashes,
  1367	        'build': {
  1368	            **BUILD_INFO_RECORD,
  1369	            'llama_server_bin': str(LLAMA_SERVER_BIN),
  1370	            'llama_server_sha256': sha256_file(LLAMA_SERVER_BIN),
  1371	            'llama_mtmd_cli_bin': str(LLAMA_MTMD_CLI_BIN),
  1372	            'llama_mtmd_cli_sha256': sha256_file(LLAMA_MTMD_CLI_BIN),
  1373	            'libggml_cpu_so': str(LIBGGML_CPU_SO),
  1374	            'libggml_cpu_so_sha256': sha256_file(LIBGGML_CPU_SO),
  1375	        },
  1376	        'weights': {
  1377	            'model_gguf': {
  1378	                'path': str(MODEL_GGUF),
  1379	                'source_repo': MODEL_SOURCE_REPO,
  1380	                'source_revision': MODEL_SOURCE_REV,
  1381	                'file_name': MODEL_GGUF.name,
  1382	                'bytes': MODEL_EXPECTED_BYTES,
  1383	                'sha256': MODEL_EXPECTED_SHA256,
  1384	            },
  1385	            'mmproj_gguf': {
  1386	                'path': str(MMPROJ_GGUF),
  1387	                'source_repo': MMPROJ_SOURCE_REPO,
  1388	                'source_revision': MMPROJ_SOURCE_REV,
  1389	                'file_name': MMPROJ_GGUF.name,
  1390	                'bytes': MMPROJ_EXPECTED_BYTES,
  1391	                'sha256': MMPROJ_EXPECTED_SHA256,
  1392	            },
  1393	        },
  1394	        'server_commands': {
  1395	            str(b): build_server_cmd(budget=b) for b in VISION_BUDGETS
  1396	        },
  1397	        'server_props': template_check['props_summary'],
  1398	        'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': peak_vmhwm_kib,
  1399	        'interpreter': {
  1400	            'executable': sys.executable,
  1401	            'python_version': sys.version.split()[0],
  1402	            'platform': sys.platform,
  1403	            'pil_version': Image.__version__,
  1404	        },
  1405	        'frames': [
  1406	            {
  1407	                'requested_stem': item['requested_stem'],
  1408	                'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
  1409	                'match_note': item['match_note'],
  1410	                'photo': item['photo'],
  1411	                'frame_path': item['frame_path'],
  1412	                'frame_sha256': item['frame_sha256'],
  1413	                'frame_bytes': item['frame_bytes'],
  1414	                'frame_size': item['frame_size'],
  1415	                'detection_count': item['detection_count'],
  1416	            }
  1417	            for item in target_frames
  1418	        ],
  1419	    }
  1420	    write_json(out_dir / 'manifest.json', manifest)
  1421	    log(f'DONE VIS-0 harness in {elapsed_s:.2f}s ({TIMING_LABEL}) -> {out_dir}')
  1422	    return out_dir
  1423	
  1424	
  1425	def main(argv=None):
  1426	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  1427	    parser.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=None, help='Process only the first N frames (smoke run)')
  1428	    parser.add_argument('--out', type=Path, default=None, help='Output directory (default: runs/<UTC>)')
  1429	    parser.add_argument('--skip-optional', action='store_true', help='Skip optional 140+YOLO pass')
  1430	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
  1431	    if args.limit is not None and args.limit < 1:
  1432	        parser.error('--limit must be >= 1')
  1433	    run_harness(limit=args.limit, out_dir=args.out, skip_optional=args.skip_optional)
  1434	
  1435	
  1436	if __name__ == '__main__':
  1437	    main()

     1	# TASK VIS-0 — Gemma 4 Template Check & Gemma Vision Feasibility (Motors Off)
     2	
     3	**Labels:** Template check VALID (deterministic); vision replies VALID as outputs (temperature 0); timings and memory NOT measurements (agent resident); quality grading pending (Local AI)
     4	
     5	## Scope
     6	
     7	1. **Step A — Template Check (text only, port 8090):**
     8	   - Saves `GET /props` `chat_template` verbatim.
     9	   - Calls `POST /tokenize` with `"with_pieces": true` on `"<start_of_turn>user"`, `"<end_of_turn>"`, `"<|turn>user"`, `"<turn|>"`, and `"<|turn>model"`, recording whether each turn marker is `ONE special token` or `several plain-text pieces`.
    10	   - Calls `POST /apply-template` with `[{"role": "user", "content": "Hi"}]` and saves the rendered prompt verbatim.
    11	   - Runs a deterministic (`temperature: 0, top_k: 1, seed: 0, max 96 tokens, cache_prompt: false`) text A/B comparison across the 10 target frames using the saved `Y-ONLY` YOLO lines from `benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z/results.json`:
    12	     - **OLD:** `POST /completion` with the exact Gemma 3 marker string (`<start_of_turn>` / `<end_of_turn>`) constructed by `chat.build_prompt('gemma', ...)`.
    13	     - **NEW:** `POST /chat/completions` with the same instruction + YOLO lines as a single `user` message so `llama-server` applies the embedded GGUF template.
    14	   - Reports `CONFIRMED` / `REFUTED` / `UNCLEAR` on whether the Gemma 3 markers are wrong for `gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf`.
    15	
    16	2. **Step B — Vision Feasibility (port 8090):**
    17	   - Evaluates the 10 640 px frames from `benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z/frames/`:
    18	     `bench_photos__blocked_1`, `bench_photos__empty_2`, `bench_photos__person_facing_150cm`, `bench_photos__person_sideon_300cm`, `bench_photos__roomsig_couch_1`, `bench_photos__roomsig_refrigerator_1`, `bench_photos__roomsig_toilet_1`, `bench_photos__roomsig_toilet_2`, `test_photos__chiara_close_back`, `test_photos__abdel_close_right`.
    19	   - Sends `POST /chat/completions` with one user message containing the JPEG as a base64 `data:image/jpeg;base64,...` URI (`image_url`) plus:
    20	     `"Describe in one or two short sentences what is in front of the robot. Mention only what you can see."`
    21	   - Tests supported Gemma 4 image token budgets `70`, `140`, and `280` via `--image-min-tokens <N> --image-max-tokens <N>` on a separate `timeout 3600` server instance on `127.0.0.1:8090` (`--threads 4 --threads-batch 4 --parallel 1 --swa-full --cache-ram 0 --ctx-size 4096 --reasoning off -lv 4`, no CPU pinning).
    22	   - If all three budgets complete without crashing, runs an **optional** pass at budget `140` with `"Objects detected by YOLO:"` plus the saved `Y-ONLY` lines appended.
    23

   154	def sha256_file(path):
   155	    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
   156	        return hashlib.file_digest(f, 'sha256').hexdigest()
   157	
   158	
   159	def verify_and_hash_imports(root=ROBOT):
   160	    """Verify every imported project module resolved inside root and return {rel_path: sha256}."""
   161	    root = Path(root).resolve()
   162	    hashes = {}
   163	    for rel_p, get_file in IMPORTED_MODULES:
   164	        expected = (root / rel_p).resolve()
   165	        actual = Path(get_file()).resolve()
   166	        if actual != expected:
   167	            raise RuntimeError(f'import origin mismatch for {rel_p}: loaded {actual}, expected {expected}')
   168	        hashes[rel_p] = sha256_file(actual)
   169	    return hashes

   176	def write_json(path, value):
   177	    path = Path(path)
   178	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   179	    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')

   182	def encode_image_data_uri(image_bytes):
   183	    """Encode raw JPEG bytes as a base64 data URI for /chat/completions image_url."""
   184	    b64 = base64.b64encode(bytes(image_bytes)).decode('ascii')
   185	    return f'data:image/jpeg;base64,{b64}'

   188	def build_server_cmd(
   189	    budget=None,
   190	    server_bin=LLAMA_SERVER_BIN,
   191	    model_path=MODEL_GGUF,
   192	    mmproj_path=MMPROJ_GGUF,
   193	    port=TEST_PORT,
   194	    ctx_size=4096,
   195	    timeout_s=SERVER_TIMEOUT_S,
   196	):
   197	    """Build the timeout-wrapped llama-server command on port 8090."""
   198	    cmd = [
   199	        'timeout',
   200	        str(timeout_s),
   201	        str(server_bin),
   202	        '-m', str(model_path),
   203	        '--mmproj', str(mmproj_path),
   204	        '--host', TEST_HOST,
   205	        '--port', str(port),
   206	        '--ctx-size', str(ctx_size),
   207	        '--threads', str(server_manager.THREADS),
   208	        '--threads-batch', str(server_manager.BATCH_THREADS),
   209	        '--parallel', '1',
   210	        '--swa-full',
   211	        '--cache-ram', '0',
   212	        '--reasoning', 'off',
   213	        '-lv', '4',
   214	    ]
   215	    if budget is not None:
   216	        cmd.extend(['--image-min-tokens', str(budget), '--image-max-tokens', str(budget)])
   217	    return cmd

   282	def classify_tokenized_marker(marker_str, tokens):
   283	    """Determine whether the turn marker tag in marker_str tokenized as ONE special token or several pieces."""
   284	    tag = marker_str[:marker_str.index('>') + 1] if '>' in marker_str else marker_str
   285	    pieces = [t.get('piece') for t in tokens]
   286	    tag_is_single = bool(tokens) and pieces[0] == tag
   287	    whole_is_single = len(tokens) == 1 and tag_is_single
   288	    if whole_is_single:
   289	        summary = 'ONE special token'
   290	    elif tag_is_single:
   291	        summary = f'ONE special token ({tag}) + role token ({len(tokens)} tokens total)'
   292	    else:
   293	        summary = f'several plain-text pieces ({len(tokens)} pieces)'
   294	    return {
   295	        'input': marker_str,
   296	        'tag': tag,
   297	        'token_count': len(tokens),
   298	        'tokens': tokens,
   299	        'pieces': pieces,
   300	        'tag_is_single_special_token': tag_is_single,
   301	        'whole_input_is_single_token': whole_is_single,
   302	        'classification': summary,
   303	    }
   304	
   305	
   306	def evaluate_template_verdict(chat_template, tokenize_results, rendered_hi):
   307	    """Return CONFIRMED / REFUTED / UNCLEAR that Gemma 3 markers are wrong for this model."""
   308	    by_input = {r['input']: r for r in tokenize_results}
   309	    gemma3_plain = (
   310	        not by_input['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   311	        and not by_input['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   312	    )
   313	    gemma4_special = (
   314	        by_input['<|turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   315	        and by_input['<turn|>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   316	        and by_input['<|turn>model']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   317	    )
   318	    template_uses_gemma4 = (
   319	        '<|turn>' in str(chat_template)
   320	        and '<turn|>' in str(chat_template)
   321	        and '<start_of_turn>' not in str(chat_template)
   322	        and '<end_of_turn>' not in str(chat_template)
   323	    )
   324	    rendered_uses_gemma4 = '<|turn>user\nHi<turn|>\n' in str(rendered_hi) and '<|turn>model\n' in str(rendered_hi)
   325	    if gemma3_plain and gemma4_special and template_uses_gemma4 and rendered_uses_gemma4:
   326	        return 'CONFIRMED'
   327	    if (
   328	        by_input['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   329	        and by_input['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   330	    ):
   331	        return 'REFUTED'
   332	    return 'UNCLEAR'
