   179	# ── Main Robot Class ──────────────────────────────────
   180	def new_slot():
   181	    return {'cycles': 0, 'frames': 0, 'detect_ms': {320: [], 640: []}, 'age_s': [], 'camera_stops': {},
   182	            'picks': 0, 'picker_ms': [], 'm2_ms': [], 'fallbacks': 0, 'ended': False}
   183	
   184	class Robot:
   185	    def __init__(self, motors=None, camera_source=CAMERA_SOURCE):
   186	        self.motors      = motors
   187	        if camera_source not in ('robotcam', 'termux_photo'):
   188	            raise ValueError(camera_source)
   189	        self.camera_source = camera_source
   190	        self.detector = None
   191	        self.size_policy = SizePolicy(LARGE_FRAME_INTERVAL_S, PERSON_HEIGHT_FRACTION)
   192	        self.camera_session = None
   193	        self.camera_frame = None
   194	        self.cycle       = 0
   195	        self.mission     = None
   196	        self.target      = None   # person name or room
   197	        self.last_moves  = []
   198	        self.scene_log   = []
   199	        self.known_rooms = {}
   200	        self.running     = False
   201	        self.state       = 'idle'  # idle / navigating / searching / found
   202	        self.heat_text   = 'heat n/a'  # skin/status for the cycle print, set by run_mission
   203	        self.slot        = new_slot()  # numbers of the current ACTIVE phase, for the 'active_slot' trace line
   204	        self.heat        = None        # the mission's HeatControl (trace lines), set by run_mission
   205	        # Picker (#109, #131). mode None = --no-picker: today's rules incl. person approach. With the picker on,
   206	        # mode is EXPLORE / APPROACH / GREET / LOOK and picker is None when it fell back at mission start.
   207	        self.picker      = None
   208	        self.mode        = None
   209	        self.mode_since  = None
   210	        self.look_cycles = 0
   211	        self.greeted     = False
   212	        self.cadence     = picker.Cadence()
   213	        self.pick_inflight = False
   214	        self.picker_severe = False
   215	        self.slot_n      = 0           # ACTIVE slot number; a pick requested in an earlier slot is not applied
   216	        self.last_640    = None        # (frame, detector dicts) of this slot's latest 640 detection, for M2
   217	
   218	    def trace(self, name, detail):
   219	        if self.heat:
   220	            self.heat.event(name, detail)
   221	
   222	    def start_camera(self, heat=None):
   223	        """heat: the mission's HeatControl, for the detector trace lines (None: no trace)."""
   224	        created = self.detector is None
   225	        # Shared by detect_scene and the robot loop. MID cores (#128); raises before `am start` when the
   226	        # ORT workers or an affinity cannot be verified, so the mission does not start.
   227	        self.detector = get_detector(pinned=True)
   228	        if created and heat:
   229	            heat.event('detector_config', {**self.detector.config(), 'camera_rate': CAMERA_RATE})
   230	        # Affinity is a power setting, not a safety stop: a failed re-pin is logged and the mission continues.
   231	        affinity = self.detector.check_affinity()
   232	        if not affinity['ok']:
   233	            print(f"[DETECTOR] affinity re-pin failed: {affinity['errors']} — continuing")
   234	        if heat:
   235	            heat.event('detector_affinity', affinity)
   236	        self.slot = new_slot()
   237	        self.slot_n += 1
   238	        self.last_640 = None
   239	        if self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
   240	            self.camera_session = None
   241	            self.camera_frame = None
   242	            started_boot_s = time.clock_gettime(time.CLOCK_BOOTTIME)
   243	            subprocess.run(['am', 'start', '-n', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam/.StartActivity',
   244	                            '--es', 'mode', 'B', '--ei', 'rate', str(CAMERA_RATE)],
   245	                           check=True, timeout=10, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
   246	                           stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   247	            deadline = time.monotonic() + 8
   248	            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   249	                result = read_frame(min_capture_boot_s=started_boot_s)
   250	                if result['status'] == 'ok':
   251	                    self.camera_session = result['session']
   252	                    return
   253	                time.sleep(0.1)
   254	            raise RuntimeError('RobotCam did not publish a usable frame')
   255	
   256	    def stop_camera(self):
   257	        if self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
   258	            subprocess.run(['am', 'broadcast', '-n', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam/.ControlReceiver',
   259	                            '-a', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam.STOP'],
   260	                           check=True, timeout=5, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
   261	                           stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   262	
   263	    def move(self, cmd, duration=CYCLE_MOVE_TIME, speed=MOTOR_SPEED):
   264	        print(f'  [MOTOR] {cmd}')
   265	        if self.motors:
   266	            if cmd == 'FORWARD':  self.motors.forward(speed, duration)
   267	            elif cmd == 'LEFT':   self.motors.rotate_left(120, duration)
   268	            elif cmd == 'RIGHT':  self.motors.rotate_right(120, duration)
   269	            elif cmd == 'BACK':   self.motors.backward(MOTOR_SPEED, duration)
   270	            elif cmd == 'STOP':   self.motors.stop()
   271	            elif cmd == 'HOLD':   # stand still for the cycle; the mission continues (GREET, person-stop)
   272	                self.motors.stop()
   273	                time.sleep(duration)
   274	            elif cmd == 'STRAFE_RIGHT': self.motors.strafe_right(100, duration)
   275	            elif cmd == 'STRAFE_LEFT':  self.motors.strafe_left(100, duration)
   276	        else:
   277	            time.sleep(duration)
   278	        self.last_moves.append(cmd)
   279	        if len(self.last_moves) > 10:
   280	            self.last_moves.pop(0)
   281	
   282	    def get_distance(self):
   283	        """cm ahead. 999 = no usable reading (treated as clear).
   284	        Firmware sends -1 for no echo, i.e. nothing within ~4m."""
   285	        if not self.motors:
   286	            return 999
   287	        d = self.motors.get_distance()
   288	        if time.time() - self.motors.state.get("dist_at", 0) > 1.0:
   289	            return 999          # stale or never received
   290	        if d < 0:
   291	            return 400          # no echo = clear to sensor max range
   292	        return d
   293	
   294	    def navigate_rules(self, results, distance, approach=True):
   295	        """Fast Python navigation — no LLM. Owns safety; Gemma never overrides.
   296	        approach=False (EXPLORE, LOOK): people are ordinary objects; no person branch.
   297	
   298	        Blocked path: strafe first (mecanum holds heading, so YOLO keeps the
   299	        same view), rotate on axis if strafing isn't clearing it, and set
   300	        nav_stuck so run_cycle hands the strategy call to Gemma.
   301	        """
   302	        self.nav_stuck = False
   303	        if not hasattr(self, 'avoid_side'):
   304	            self.avoid_side = 'LEFT'
   305	        if not hasattr(self, 'blocked_n'):
   306	            self.blocked_n = 0
   307	        if not hasattr(self, 'asked_gemma'):
   308	            self.asked_gemma = False
   309	        if not hasattr(self, 'blocked_since'):
   310	            self.blocked_since = None
   311	
   312	        ESCAPE_TIMEOUT = 60.0   # s of continuous blockage before giving up
   313	
   314	        if distance < OBSTACLE_DIST and self.blocked_since is None:
   315	            self.blocked_since = time.time()
   316	
   317	        # Very close: rotate to sweep the sensor, but give up on the same
   318	        # timeout as the main ladder rather than turning forever.
   319	        if distance < 15:
   320	            self.blocked_n += 1
   321	            if time.time() - self.blocked_since >= ESCAPE_TIMEOUT:
   322	                print(f'  [NAV] cornered for {ESCAPE_TIMEOUT:.0f}s — giving up')
   323	                return 'STOP'
   324	            return self.avoid_side
   325	
   326	        # ── Safety ──────────────────────────────────────────
   327	        if distance < OBSTACLE_DIST:
   328	            self.blocked_n += 1
   329	            n = self.blocked_n
   330	
   331	            if n <= 2:                      # strafe — holds heading for YOLO
   332	                return 'STRAFE_' + self.avoid_side
   333	            if n <= 6:                      # sweep this side, sensor leads
   334	                return self.avoid_side
   335	            if n == 7:                      # committed flip, ask Gemma once
   336	                self.avoid_side = 'RIGHT' if self.avoid_side == 'LEFT' else 'LEFT'
   337	                if not self.asked_gemma:
   338	                    self.nav_stuck = True
   339	                    self.asked_gemma = True
   340	                return self.avoid_side
   341	            if n <= 13:                     # sweep back through and past centre
   342	                return self.avoid_side
   343	
   344	            # A full sweep found nothing. Keep trying until the timeout, then
   345	            # declare defeat rather than spinning indefinitely.
   346	            if time.time() - self.blocked_since < ESCAPE_TIMEOUT:
   347	                self.blocked_n = 0          # restart the ladder
   348	                return 'STRAFE_' + self.avoid_side
   349	            print(f'  [NAV] boxed in for {ESCAPE_TIMEOUT:.0f}s — giving up')
   350	            return 'STOP'
   351	
   352	        # path is clear — reset the avoidance state machine
   353	        self.blocked_n = 0
   354	        self.asked_gemma = False
   355	        self.blocked_since = None
   356	
   357	        # ── Person ──────────────────────────────────────────
   358	        # Person-stop (#79) is applied to the final move in choose_move(), in every mode.
   359	        labels = [r[0] for r in results]
   360	        if approach and 'person' in labels:
   361	            direction = person_direction(results)
   362	            if direction:
   363	                return direction
   364	
   365	        # ── Room signature ──────────────────────────────────
   366	        if 'refrigerator' in labels:
   367	            room = 'kitchen'
   368	        elif 'couch' in labels or 'tv' in labels:
   369	            room = 'living room'
   370	        elif 'bed' in labels:
   371	            room = 'bedroom'
   372	        elif 'toilet' in labels:
   373	            room = 'bathroom'
   374	        else:
   375	            room = None
   376	
   377	        if room and room not in self.known_rooms:
   378	            self.known_rooms[room] = time.strftime('%H:%M')
   379	            print(f'  [MAP] Found: {room}')
   380	
   381	        return 'FORWARD'
   382	
   383	    def run_cycle(self, photo_path=None):
   384	        self.cycle += 1
   385	        self.slot['cycles'] += 1
   386	        self.collect_pick()  # a pick that finished meanwhile changes the mode from this cycle on
   387	        age = None
   388	        distance = self.get_distance()
   389	        print(f'\n── Cycle {self.cycle} | {self.heat_text} | dist:{distance}cm ──')
   390	
   391	        # Vision: every cycle must have a fresh usable frame before any movement.
   392	        if photo_path:
   393	            path = photo_path
   394	        elif self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
   395	            frame_result = read_frame(session=self.camera_session)
   396	            if frame_result['status'] != 'ok' or (self.camera_frame is not None and
   397	                    frame_result['frame'] <= self.camera_frame):
   398	                reason = frame_result['status'] if frame_result['status'] != 'ok' else 'no new'
   399	                print(f'  [CAM] {reason} frame — stop')
   400	                self.slot['camera_stops'][reason] = self.slot['camera_stops'].get(reason, 0) + 1
   401	                if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
   402	                return 'STOP'
   403	            self.camera_session = frame_result['session']
   404	            self.camera_frame = frame_result['frame']
   405	            frame = frame_result['image']
   406	            age = frame_result['age_s']
   407	            self.slot['frames'] += 1
   408	            self.slot['age_s'].append(age)
   409	        else:
   410	            if not take_photo(PHOTO_A):
   411	                print('  [CAM] Photo failed')
   412	                if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
   413	                return 'STOP'
   414	            path = PHOTO_A
   415	
   416	        detections = []
   417	        try:
   418	            if photo_path:
   419	                results = detect_scene(path)
   420	            else:
   421	                if self.detector is None: self.detector = get_detector()
   422	                size = self.size_policy.next_size()
   423	                if self.camera_source == 'termux_photo':
   424	                    from PIL import Image, ImageOps
   425	                    with Image.open(path) as source:
   426	                        frame = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert('RGB')
   427	                started = time.perf_counter()
   428	                detections = self.detector.detect(frame, size)
   429	                detect_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
   430	                self.slot['detect_ms'][size].append(detect_ms)
   431	                self.size_policy.observe(detections, frame.height)
   432	                results = scene_from_detections(detections, *frame.size)
   433	                if size == 640:
   434	                    self.last_640 = (frame, detections)
   435	                print(f'  [YOLO] size={size} person={any(d["class_name"] == "person" for d in detections)} '
   436	                      f'detect={detect_ms:.0f}ms age={"n/a" if age is None else f"{age:.2f}s"}')
   437	        except Exception as exc:
   438	            print(f'  [VISION] {exc} — stop')
   439	            if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
   440	            return 'STOP'
   441	        scene = scene_to_text(results)
   442	        self.scene_log.append(scene)
   443	        if len(self.scene_log) > 10:
   444	            self.scene_log.pop(0)
   445	        print(f'  [YOLO] {scene}')
   446	
   447	        # Fast navigation rules, shaped by the mode; person-stop last. The legacy tuples keep one person (the most
   448	        # confident); person-stop also sees every other person detection's area bucket.
   449	        people = [r[3] for r in results if r[0] == 'person'] + [
   450	            scene_from_detections([d], *frame.size)[0][3] for d in detections if d['class_name'] == 'person']
   451	        move = self.choose_move(results, distance, people)
   452	        print(f'  [NAV] {move}')
   453	        self.request_pick(results, distance)  # never waits; M2 runs during the move below
   454	
   455	        # Execute move (motors cool during movement)
   456	        if move != 'STOP':
   457	            self.move(move)
   458	        else:
   459	            print('  [STOP] Mission complete or waiting')
   460	            self.state = 'found'
   461	
   462	        return move
   463	
   464	    def choose_move(self, results, distance, people=None):
   465	        """The mode only shapes the Python rules (#104/#109); obstacle rules stay inside navigate_rules. Person-stop
   466	        (#79) applies in every mode: a forward move while any person's area bucket is in PERSON_STOP_BUCKETS holds.
   467	        people: the area bucket of every person detection (default: the person tuples in results)."""
   468	        if people is None:
   469	            people = [r[3] for r in results if r[0] == 'person']
   470	        if self.mode == 'GREET':  # stand still until the next pick; greet once per GREET
   471	            if not self.greeted:
   472	                speak(GREETING)
   473	                self.greeted = True
   474	            move = 'HOLD'
   475	        else:
   476	            move = self.navigate_rules(results, distance, approach=self.mode in (None, 'APPROACH'))
   477	            if self.mode == 'LOOK':
   478	                if move == 'FORWARD':  # clear path: rotate in place instead
   479	                    move = 'LEFT'
   480	                self.look_cycles += 1
   481	        close = [b for b in people if b in PERSON_STOP_BUCKETS]
   482	        if move in FORWARD_MOVES and close:
   483	            print(f'  [PERSON] {close[0]} person — {move} held')
   484	            self.trace('person_stop', {'move': move, 'mode': self.mode, 'bucket': close[0]})
   485	            move = 'HOLD'
   486	        if self.mode == 'LOOK' and self.look_cycles >= LOOK_CYCLES:
   487	            self.set_mode('EXPLORE', f'LOOK done after {LOOK_CYCLES} cycles')
   488	        return move
   489	
   490	    def set_mode(self, mode, reason):
   491	        if mode == self.mode:
   492	            return
   493	        now = time.monotonic()
   494	        print(f'  [MODE] {self.mode} -> {mode}: {reason}')
   495	        self.trace('mode', {'from': self.mode, 'to': mode, 'reason': reason,
   496	                            'after_s': None if self.mode_since is None else round(now - self.mode_since, 1)})
   497	        self.mode, self.mode_since, self.look_cycles, self.greeted = mode, now, 0, False
   498	
   499	    def request_pick(self, results, distance):
   500	        """Start a pick in the worker when the cadence says so (owner D2); ACTIVE cycles only, never at Android
   501	        status SEVERE or above (#127: no optional Gemma work), at most one in flight, only with a 640 frame."""
   502	        if self.picker is None:
   503	            return
   504	        severe = self.severe_now()
   505	        (self.picker.forbid if severe else self.picker.allow)()  # also cancels a pick already running
   506	        if severe != self.picker_severe:
   507	            self.picker_severe = severe
   508	            print(f"  [PICKER] {'no picks at status SEVERE or above; mode stays ' + self.mode if severe else 'picks resume'}")
   509	            self.trace('picker_severe', {'severe': severe, 'mode': self.mode})
   510	        person = any(r[0] == 'person' for r in results)
   511	        now = time.monotonic()
   512	        trigger = self.cadence.due(now, person, distance < OBSTACLE_DIST,
   513	                                   ready=not (self.pick_inflight or severe or self.last_640 is None))
   514	        if trigger is None:
   515	            return
   516	        self.picker.submit({
   517	            'slot': self.slot_n, 'trigger': trigger, 'eligible': picker.eligible(person), 'frame_640': self.last_640,
   518	            'situation': picker.situation(self.mission, results, distance, self.mode, now - self.mode_since,
   519	                                          self.last_moves, list(self.known_rooms))})
   520	        self.pick_inflight = True
   521	
   522	    def severe_now(self):
   523	        """The latest Android status is SEVERE or above (#127: no optional Gemma work)."""
   524	        dump = self.heat.monitor.readings()['dump'] if self.heat else None
   525	        return bool(dump) and dump['status'] >= STATUS_SEVERE
   526	
   527	    def collect_pick(self):
   528	        """Trace a finished pick (any phase, and at mission end). Its numbers count only in its own ACTIVE slot while
   529	        that slot is open; its mode applies only then, below SEVERE, and when it was not cancelled. A failed pick is
   530	        EXPLORE: the fallback, never a stop."""
   531	        if self.picker is None or not self.pick_inflight:
   532	            return
   533	        r = self.picker.result()
   534	        if r is None:
   535	            return
   536	        self.pick_inflight = False
   537	        own_slot = r['slot'] == self.slot_n and not self.slot['ended']
   538	        applied = own_slot and not r['cancelled'] and not self.severe_now()  # the reading now, not last cycle's
   539	        if own_slot:
   540	            self.slot['picks'] += 1
   541	            self.slot['fallbacks'] += r['fallback'] is not None
   542	            for key in ('picker_ms', 'm2_ms'):
   543	                if r[key] is not None:
   544	                    self.slot[key].append(r[key])
   545	        self.trace('pick', {**r, 'applied': applied})
   546	        if r['fallback']:
   547	            print(f"  [PICKER] fallback to EXPLORE: {r['fallback']}")
   548	        if applied:
   549	            self.set_mode(r['mode'], f"pick ({r['trigger']})" if r['fallback'] is None else 'picker fallback')
   550	
   551	    def pause_cycle(self):
   552	        """Planned heat pause (#129): camera off, no YOLO; ultrasonic-only rules."""
   553	        self.cycle += 1
   554	        distance = self.get_distance()
   555	        print(f'\n── Pause cycle {self.cycle} | {self.heat_text} | dist:{distance}cm ──')
   556	        if distance == 999:     # stale or no reading: never drive blind, rotate in place
   557	            move = getattr(self, 'avoid_side', 'LEFT')
   558	        else:
   559	            move = self.navigate_rules([], distance)
   560	        print(f'  [NAV] {move}')
   561	        if move != 'STOP':
   562	            self.move(move, speed=PAUSE_FORWARD_SPEED)  # speed applies to FORWARD only
   563	        else:
   564	            print('  [STOP] Mission complete or waiting')
   565	            self.state = 'found'
   566	        return move
   567	
   568	    def run_mission(self, mission_text, heat=None):
   569	        """Run until mission complete or stopped, under heat control (#127, #129).
   570	        heat: a started heat_control.HeatControl; the camera is already on (ACTIVE)."""
   571	        if heat is None:
   572	            raise ValueError('run_mission needs heat control (heat_control.HeatControl)')
   573	        self.mission = mission_text
   574	        self.state = 'navigating'
   575	        self.running = True
   576	        self.heat = heat
   577	        if self.picker:
   578	            self.picker.allow()
   579	        print(f'\n[ROBOT] Mission: {mission_text}')
   580	
   581	        try:
   582	            while self.running and self.state != 'found':
   583	                self.collect_pick()  # also in PAUSE/HOLD: every pick is traced when it ends
   584	                target, reason = heat.decide(self.get_distance())
   585	                if self.picker and self.severe_now():
   586	                    self.picker.forbid()  # before this cycle's vision: no further picker request at SEVERE
   587	                self.heat_text = heat.summary()
   588	                if target == 'END':
   589	                    print(f'[HEAT] END: {reason}')
   590	                    break
   591	                if target != heat.phase:
   592	                    print(f'[HEAT] {heat.phase} -> {target}: {reason}')
   593	                    if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE':      # into PAUSE or HOLD: camera off, detector stays loaded
   594	                        if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
   595	                        if self.picker: self.picker.forbid()  # no M2 or Gemma outside ACTIVE (#129)
   596	                        try:
   597	                            self.stop_camera()
   598	                        except Exception as exc:
   599	                            print(f'[CAM] stop failed: {exc} — mission ends')
   600	                            heat.event('camera_stop_fail', str(exc))
   601	                            break
   602	                        heat.event('camera_stop_ok')
   603	                        self.end_slot(heat)
   604	                        heat.enter(target, since=heat.now())
   605	                    elif target == 'ACTIVE':
   606	                        started = heat.now()
   607	                        try:
   608	                            self.start_camera(heat)  # resets the frame counter, session and slot numbers
   609	                        except Exception as exc:
   610	                            print(f'[CAM] start failed: {exc} — mission ends')
   611	                            heat.event('camera_start_fail', str(exc))
   612	                            break
   613	                        heat.event('camera_start_ok')
   614	                        heat.enter('ACTIVE', since=started)
   615	                        if self.picker: self.picker.allow()
   616	                    else:                           # PAUSE <-> HOLD; camera already off
   617	                        heat.enter(target)
   618	
   619	                if heat.phase == 'HOLD':
   620	                    if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
   621	                    time.sleep(1)
   622	                    continue
   623	                move = self.run_cycle() if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE' else self.pause_cycle()
   624	
   625	                if move == 'STOP':
   626	                    break
   627	        finally:                                # also on Ctrl-C, SIGTERM/SIGHUP (SystemExit) and errors
   628	            if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE':
   629	                self.end_slot(heat)
   630	        print('[ROBOT] Mission ended')
   631	        if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
   632	
   633	    def end_slot(self, heat):
   634	        """One 'active_slot' trace line: the ACTIVE phase that just ended (into PAUSE/HOLD, or mission end).
   635	        At most once per slot."""
   636	        s = self.slot
   637	        if s['ended']:
   638	            return
   639	        s['ended'] = True
   640	        def stats(values):
   641	            return {'median': round(statistics.median(values), 3), 'max': round(max(values), 3)} if values else None
   642	        heat.event('active_slot', {
   643	            'cycles': s['cycles'], 'frames': s['frames'],
   644	            'detections': {size: len(ms) for size, ms in s['detect_ms'].items()},
   645	            'detect_ms': {size: stats(ms) for size, ms in s['detect_ms'].items()},
   646	            'age_s': stats(s['age_s']), 'camera_stops': s['camera_stops'],
   647	            'affinity': self.detector.affinity() if self.detector else None,
   648	            'picks': s['picks'], 'picker_ms': stats(s['picker_ms']), 'm2_ms': stats(s['m2_ms']),
   649	            'fallbacks': s['fallbacks']})
   650	
   651	    def stereo_depth_scan(self):
   652	        """Strafe 5cm right, take two photos, return depth data."""
   653	        print('[STEREO] Starting depth scan...')
   654	        take_photo(PHOTO_A)
   655	        self.move('STRAFE_RIGHT', duration=0.3)  # ~5cm
   656	        time.sleep(0.3)
   657	        take_photo(PHOTO_B)
   658	        self.move('STRAFE_LEFT', duration=0.3)   # return
   659	        enhanced = stereo_scan(PHOTO_A, PHOTO_B, STEREO_BASELINE)
   660	        depth_scene = scene_with_depth(enhanced)
   661	        print(f'[STEREO] {depth_scene}')
   662	        return enhanced, depth_scene
   663	
   664	    def voice_command(self):
   665	        """Listen for voice command and parse it."""
   666	        speak('Ready')
   667	        text = listen()
   668	        print(f'[VOICE] Heard: {text}')
   669	        if not text:
   670	            return []
   671	        actions = parse_command(text)
   672	        print(f'[VOICE] Actions: {actions}')
   673	        return actions
   674	
   675	if __name__ == '__main__':
   676	    print('Robot system initialized')
   677	    print('Testing scene detection...')

   160	+def sha256_file(path):
   161	+    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
   162	+        return hashlib.file_digest(f, 'sha256').hexdigest()

   165	+def verify_and_hash_imports(root=ROBOT):
   166	+    """Verify every imported project module resolved inside root and return {rel_path: sha256}."""
   167	+    root = Path(root).resolve()
   168	+    hashes = {}
   169	+    for rel_p, get_file in IMPORTED_MODULES:
   170	+        expected = (root / rel_p).resolve()
   171	+        actual = Path(get_file()).resolve()
   172	+        if actual != expected:
   173	+            raise RuntimeError(f'import origin mismatch for {rel_p}: loaded {actual}, expected {expected}')
   174	+        hashes[rel_p] = sha256_file(actual)
   175	+    return hashes

   194	+def build_server_cmd(

   288	+def classify_tokenized_marker(marker_str, tokens):
   289	+    """Determine whether the turn marker tag in marker_str tokenized as ONE special token or several pieces."""
   290	+    tag = marker_str[:marker_str.index('>') + 1] if '>' in marker_str else marker_str
   291	+    pieces = [t.get('piece') for t in tokens]
   292	+    tag_is_single = bool(tokens) and pieces[0] == tag
   293	+    whole_is_single = len(tokens) == 1 and tag_is_single
   294	+    if whole_is_single:
   295	+        summary = 'ONE special token'
   296	+    elif tag_is_single:
   297	+        summary = f'ONE special token ({tag}) + role token ({len(tokens)} tokens total)'
   298	+    else:
   299	+        summary = f'several plain-text pieces ({len(tokens)} pieces)'
   300	+    return {
   301	+        'input': marker_str,
   302	+        'tag': tag,
   303	+        'token_count': len(tokens),
   304	+        'tokens': tokens,
   305	+        'pieces': pieces,
   306	+        'tag_is_single_special_token': tag_is_single,
   307	+        'whole_input_is_single_token': whole_is_single,
   308	+        'classification': summary,
   309	+    }
   310	+
   311	+
   312	+def evaluate_template_verdict(chat_template, tokenize_results, rendered_hi):
   313	+    """Return CONFIRMED / REFUTED / UNCLEAR that Gemma 3 markers are wrong for this model."""
   314	+    by_input = {r['input']: r for r in tokenize_results}
   315	+    gemma3_plain = (
   316	+        not by_input['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   317	+        and not by_input['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   318	+    )
   319	+    gemma4_special = (
   320	+        by_input['<|turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   321	+        and by_input['<turn|>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   322	+        and by_input['<|turn>model']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   323	+    )
   324	+    template_uses_gemma4 = (
   325	+        '<|turn>' in str(chat_template)
   326	+        and '<turn|>' in str(chat_template)
   327	+        and '<start_of_turn>' not in str(chat_template)
   328	+        and '<end_of_turn>' not in str(chat_template)
   329	+    )
   330	+    rendered_uses_gemma4 = '<|turn>user\nHi<turn|>\n' in str(rendered_hi) and '<|turn>model\n' in str(rendered_hi)
   331	+    if gemma3_plain and gemma4_special and template_uses_gemma4 and rendered_uses_gemma4:
   332	+        return 'CONFIRMED'
   333	+    if (
   334	+        by_input['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   335	+        and by_input['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token']
   336	+    ):
   337	+        return 'REFUTED'
   338	+    return 'UNCLEAR'
   339	+
   340	+
   341	+def extract_yolo_lines_from_m2q1_record(rec):
   342	+    """Extract the Y-ONLY YOLO lines and verify chat.build_prompt reproduces M2-Q1's exact Y-ONLY prompt."""
   343	+    old_prompt = rec['gemma_prompts']['Y-ONLY']
   344	+    used_dets = rec.get('m2_detections') or rec.get('detections') or []
   345	+    yolo_lines = '\n'.join(
   346	+        f"- {d['class_name']} [{i}], {d['position']}, {d['distance']}"
   347	+        for i, d in enumerate(used_dets)
   348	+    )
   349	+    user_text = f'{TEXT_INSTRUCTION}\n\n{yolo_lines}'
   350	+    rebuilt = build_prompt('gemma', [{'role': 'user', 'content': user_text}])
   351	+    if rebuilt != old_prompt:
   352	+        raise RuntimeError(f"M2-Q1 Y-ONLY prompt mismatch for {rec.get('photo')}")
   353	+    return yolo_lines, user_text, old_prompt
   354	+
   355	+
   356	+def load_target_frames(
   357	+    results_json_path=M2Q1_RESULTS_JSON,
   358	+    frames_dir=M2Q1_FRAMES_DIR,
   359	+    limit=None,
   360	+    stems=FRAME_STEMS,
   361	+):
   362	+    """Load the target frames, enforcing exact frame identity and 640 px JPEG resolution."""
   363	+    with open(results_json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
   364	+        m2q1 = json.load(f)
   365	+    by_stem = {}
   366	+    for rec in m2q1.get('photos', []):
   367	+        frame_name = Path(rec['frame_file']).name
   368	+        stem = frame_name[:-4] if frame_name.endswith('.jpg') else frame_name
   369	+        by_stem[stem] = rec
   370	+
   371	+    selected_stems = stems[:limit] if limit is not None else stems
   372	+    items = []
   373	+    for wanted in selected_stems:
   374	+        if wanted not in by_stem:
   375	+            raise RuntimeError(f'Required frame {wanted!r} not found in {results_json_path}')
   376	+        matched_stem = wanted
   377	+        rec = by_stem[matched_stem]
   378	+        frame_path = Path(frames_dir) / f'{matched_stem}.jpg'
   379	+        if not frame_path.is_file():
   380	+            raise RuntimeError(f'Frame file missing: {frame_path}')
   381	+        yolo_lines, user_text, old_prompt = extract_yolo_lines_from_m2q1_record(rec)
   382	+        frame_bytes = frame_path.read_bytes()
   383	+        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(frame_bytes)) as img_check:
   384	+            actual_size = [int(img_check.width), int(img_check.height)]
   385	+        if max(actual_size) != 640 or min(actual_size) <= 0:
   386	+            raise RuntimeError(
   387	+                f'Frame {frame_path} has dimensions {actual_size}, expected 640 px longest edge'
   388	+            )
   389	+        recorded_size = rec.get('frame_size')
   390	+        if recorded_size is not None and [int(recorded_size[0]), int(recorded_size[1])] != actual_size:
   391	+            raise RuntimeError(
   392	+                f'Frame size mismatch for {matched_stem}: results.json has {recorded_size}, JPEG is {actual_size}'
   393	+            )
   394	+        frame_sha = hashlib.sha256(frame_bytes).hexdigest()
   395	+        items.append({
   396	+            'requested_stem': wanted,
   397	+            'frame_stem': matched_stem,
   398	+            'match_note': 'exact match',
   399	+            'photo': rec['photo'],
   400	+            'frame_path': str(frame_path),
   401	+            'frame_sha256': frame_sha,
   402	+            'frame_bytes': len(frame_bytes),
   403	+            'frame_size': actual_size,
   404	+            'detection_count': rec.get('detection_count'),
   405	+            'yolo_lines': yolo_lines,
   406	+            'user_text': user_text,
   407	+            'old_prompt': old_prompt,
   408	+            'm2q1_replies': rec.get('replies'),
   409	+        })
   410	+    return items
   411	+
   412	+
   413	+def _parse_log_ts_ms(ts_str):
   414	+    parts = [int(p) for p in ts_str.split('.')]
   415	+    if len(parts) != 4:
   416	+        return None
   417	+    mins, secs, ms, us = parts
   418	+    return ((mins * 60 + secs) * 1000.0) + ms + (us / 1000.0)
   419	+
   420	+
   421	+def parse_server_log_delta(log_delta):
   422	+    """Extract logged image token counts and image-encode time (if printed) from a server log slice."""
   423	+    batch_tokens = [
   424	+        int(m) for m in re.findall(r'decoding image batch \d+/\d+, n_tokens_batch = (\d+)', log_delta)
   425	+    ]
   426	+    enc_batch_tokens = [
   427	+        int(m) for m in re.findall(r'encoding batch with \d+ entries and total (\d+) tokens', log_delta)
   428	+    ]
   429	+    sampler_tokens = [
   430	+        int(tot) - int(txt)
   431	+        for txt, tot in re.findall(r'init sampler, took [0-9.]+ ms, tokens: text = (\d+), total = (\d+)', log_delta)
   432	+        if int(tot) > int(txt)
   433	+    ]
   434	+    if batch_tokens:
   435	+        image_tokens = sum(batch_tokens)
   436	+    elif enc_batch_tokens:
   437	+        image_tokens = enc_batch_tokens[-1]
   438	+    elif sampler_tokens:
   439	+        image_tokens = sampler_tokens[-1]
   440	+    else:
   441	+        image_tokens = None
   442	+
   443	+    encode_ms = None
   444	+    for pat in (
   445	+        r'image_encode[^0-9\n]*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   446	+        r'slice\s+encoded\s+in\s+([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   447	+        r'encoded\s+in\s+([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   448	+        r'encode_mtmd_chunk[^0-9\n]*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*ms',
   449	+    ):
   450	+        m = re.search(pat, log_delta, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
   451	+        if m:
   452	+            encode_ms = float(m.group(1))
   453	+            break
   454	+
   455	+    if encode_ms is None:
   456	+        m_enc = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d{2}\.\d{3}\.\d{3})\s+.*encoding mtmd batch from idx', log_delta)
   457	+        m_dec = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d{2}\.\d{3}\.\d{3})\s+.*decoding image batch 1/', log_delta)
   458	+        if m_enc and m_dec:
   459	+            t1 = _parse_log_ts_ms(m_enc.group(1))
   460	+            t2 = _parse_log_ts_ms(m_dec.group(1))
   461	+            if t1 is not None and t2 is not None and t2 >= t1:
   462	+                encode_ms = round(t2 - t1, 3)
   463	+
   464	+    return {
   465	+        'image_tokens_logged': image_tokens,
   466	+        'image_tokens_batches': batch_tokens,
   467	+        'image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement': encode_ms,
   468	+    }
   469	+
   470	+
   471	+def read_vmhwm_kib_for_tree(timeout_pid):
   472	+    """Read VmHWM (in KiB) from /proc/<pid>/status for timeout_pid and any direct child (llama-server)."""
   473	+    best_kib = None
   474	+    best_pid = None
   475	+    best_name = None
   476	+    for status_path in glob.glob('/proc/[0-9]*/status'):
   477	+        try:
   478	+            pid = int(status_path.split('/')[2])
   479	+            text = Path(status_path).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
   480	+        except OSError:
   481	+            continue
   482	+        ppid = None
   483	+        name = None
   484	+        hwm_kib = None
   485	+        for line in text.splitlines():
   486	+            if line.startswith('Name:'):
   487	+                name = line.split(':', 1)[1].strip()
   488	+            elif line.startswith('PPid:'):
   489	+                try:
   490	+                    ppid = int(line.split(':', 1)[1].strip())
   491	+                except ValueError:
   492	+                    pass
   493	+            elif line.startswith('VmHWM:'):
   494	+                parts = line.split()
   495	+                if len(parts) >= 2 and parts[1].isdigit():
   496	+                    hwm_kib = int(parts[1])
   497	+        if pid == timeout_pid or ppid == timeout_pid:
   498	+            if hwm_kib is not None and (best_kib is None or hwm_kib > best_kib):
   499	+                best_kib = hwm_kib
   500	+                best_pid = pid
   501	+                best_name = name
   502	+    return {'pid': best_pid, 'name': best_name, 'vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': best_kib}
   503	+
   504	+
   505	+def check_port_8080_stopped(port=ROBOT_PORT):
   506	+    """Ensure the robot server on port 8080 is stopped before starting a test server on 8090."""
   507	+    try:
   508	+        with urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health', timeout=2) as resp:
   509	+            body = resp.read().decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
   510	+            raise RuntimeError(
   511	+                f'STOP: robot server on 127.0.0.1:{port} is still running ({resp.status}: {body}). '
   512	+                'Owner must stop it before running VIS-0.'
   513	+            )
   514	+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
   515	+        body = ''
   516	+        if e.fp is not None:
   517	+            try:
   518	+                body = e.fp.read().decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
   519	+            except Exception:
   520	+                body = ''
   521	+        raise RuntimeError(
   522	+            f'STOP: robot server on 127.0.0.1:{port} responded with HTTP {e.code} ({e.reason}: {body}). '
   523	+            'Owner must stop it before running VIS-0.'
   524	+        ) from e
   525	+    except urllib.error.URLError as e:
   526	+        reason = getattr(e, 'reason', e)
   527	+        if not isinstance(reason, ConnectionRefusedError):
   528	+            raise RuntimeError(
   529	+                f'STOP: could not confirm 127.0.0.1:{port} is stopped (unexpected URLError: {e}). '
   530	+                'Owner must ensure port 8080 is down before running VIS-0.'
   531	+            ) from e
   532	+    check_port_free(port)
   533	+    return True
   534	+
   535	+
   536	+def check_port_free(port=TEST_PORT):
   537	+    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
   538	+        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
   539	+        try:
   540	+            sock.bind((TEST_HOST, port))
   541	+        except OSError as e:
   542	+            raise RuntimeError(f'Port {TEST_HOST}:{port} is already in use: {e}') from e
   543	+
   544	+
   545	+def http_json(path, body=None, base_url=TEST_URL, timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT_S):
   546	+    data = None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode('utf-8')
   547	+    req = urllib.request.Request(
   548	+        base_url + path,
   549	+        data=data,
   550	+        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   551	+        method='GET' if data is None else 'POST',
   552	+    )
   553	+    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
   554	+        return json.loads(resp.read().decode('utf-8'))
   555	+
   556	+
   557	+def extract_chat_reply(resp_json):
   558	+    """Extract assistant reply content, reasoning_content, finish_reason, usage, and timings from /chat/completions."""
   559	+    choices = resp_json.get('choices') or []
   560	+    if not choices:
   561	+        raise RuntimeError(f'No choices in /chat/completions response: {resp_json}')
   562	+    choice0 = choices[0]
   563	+    msg = choice0.get('message') or {}
   564	+    raw_content = msg.get('content')
   565	+    reasoning_content = msg.get('reasoning_content')
   566	+    content = '' if raw_content is None else str(raw_content).strip()
   567	+    finish_reason = choice0.get('finish_reason')
   568	+    usage = resp_json.get('usage') or {}
   569	+    timings = resp_json.get('timings') or {}
   570	+    prompt_tokens = usage.get('prompt_tokens')
   571	+    if prompt_tokens is None:
   572	+        prompt_tokens = timings.get('prompt_n')
   573	+    completion_tokens = usage.get('completion_tokens')
   574	+    if completion_tokens is None:
   575	+        completion_tokens = timings.get('predicted_n')
   576	+    return {
   577	+        'reply': content,
   578	+        'raw_content': raw_content,
   579	+        'reasoning_content': reasoning_content,
   580	+        'finish_reason': finish_reason,
   581	+        'prompt_tokens': prompt_tokens,
   582	+        'completion_tokens': completion_tokens,
   583	+        'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('prompt_ms'),
   584	+        'predicted_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_ms'),
   585	+        'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_per_second'),
   586	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   587	+    }
   588	+
   589	+
   590	+def extract_completion_reply(resp_json):
   591	+    """Extract reply content and timings from /completion."""
   592	+    if 'content' not in resp_json:
   593	+        raise RuntimeError(f'Unexpected /completion response: {resp_json}')
   594	+    raw_content = str(resp_json['content'])
   595	+    timings = resp_json.get('timings') or {}
   596	+    return {
   597	+        'reply': raw_content.strip(),
   598	+        'raw_content': raw_content,
   599	+        'stop_type': resp_json.get('stop_type'),
   600	+        'stopping_word': resp_json.get('stopping_word'),
   601	+        'tokens_evaluated': resp_json.get('tokens_evaluated', timings.get('prompt_n')),
   602	+        'tokens_predicted': resp_json.get('tokens_predicted', timings.get('predicted_n')),
   603	+        'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('prompt_ms'),
   604	+        'predicted_ms_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_ms'),
   605	+        'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': timings.get('predicted_per_second'),
   606	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   607	+    }
   608	+
   609	+
   610	+def format_server_log_banner(started_utc, label, budget, cmd):
   611	+    return (
   612	+        f"\n{'=' * 72}\n"
   613	+        f"[{started_utc}] START {label} (budget={budget})\n"
   614	+        f"LABELS: {REPORT_LABELS}\n"
   615	+        f"TIMINGS/MEMORY: {TIMING_LABEL}\n"
   616	+        f"CMD: {' '.join(cmd)}\n"
   617	+        f"{'=' * 72}\n"
   618	+    )
   619	+
   620	+
   621	+class ManagedTestServer:
   622	+    """Manages a single timeout-wrapped llama-server instance on port 8090."""
   623	+
   624	+    def __init__(self, budget, server_log_path, label=''):
   625	+        self.budget = budget
   626	+        self.server_log_path = Path(server_log_path)
   627	+        self.label = label or f'budget_{budget}'
   628	+        self.cmd = build_server_cmd(budget=budget)
   629	+        self.proc = None
   630	+        self.log_handle = None
   631	+        self.peak_vmhwm_kib = None
   632	+        self.server_pid = None
   633	+        self.server_name = None
   634	+        self.started_utc = None
   635	+        self.ended_utc = None
   636	+        self.startup_s_not_a_measurement = None
   637	+        self.exit_code = None
   638	+
   639	+    def sample_vmhwm(self):
   640	+        if self.proc is None:
   641	+            return None
   642	+        info = read_vmhwm_kib_for_tree(self.proc.pid)
   643	+        hwm = info['vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement']
   644	+        if hwm is not None and (self.peak_vmhwm_kib is None or hwm > self.peak_vmhwm_kib):
   645	+            self.peak_vmhwm_kib = hwm
   646	+            self.server_pid = info['pid']
   647	+            self.server_name = info['name']
   648	+        return self.peak_vmhwm_kib
   649	+
   650	+    def start(self):
   651	+        check_port_8080_stopped(ROBOT_PORT)
   652	+        check_port_free(TEST_PORT)
   653	+        self.server_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   654	+        self.started_utc = utc_now()
   655	+        banner = format_server_log_banner(self.started_utc, self.label, self.budget, self.cmd)
   656	+        with open(self.server_log_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as lf:
   657	+            lf.write(banner)
   658	+        self.log_handle = open(self.server_log_path, 'ab')
   659	+        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith('LLAMA_ARG_')}
   660	+        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   661	+        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(
   662	+            self.cmd,
   663	+            env=env,
   664	+            stdout=self.log_handle,
   665	+            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   666	+        )
   667	+        deadline = time.perf_counter() + STARTUP_TIMEOUT_S
   668	+        while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
   669	+            if self.proc.poll() is not None:
   670	+                self.exit_code = self.proc.returncode
   671	+                raise RuntimeError(
   672	+                    f'llama-server exited during startup with code {self.exit_code}; '
   673	+                    f'tail:\n{self.log_tail(25)}'
   674	+                )
   675	+            try:
   676	+                health = http_json('/health', timeout=2)
   677	+                if health.get('status') == 'ok':
   678	+                    self.startup_s_not_a_measurement = time.perf_counter() - t0
   679	+                    self.sample_vmhwm()
   680	+                    return
   681	+            except Exception:
   682	+                pass
   683	+            time.sleep(0.5)
   684	+        self.stop()
   685	+        raise TimeoutError(f'llama-server did not become healthy within {STARTUP_TIMEOUT_S}s')
   686	+
   687	+    def is_alive(self):
   688	+        return self.proc is not None and self.proc.poll() is None
   689	+
   690	+    def log_size(self):
   691	+        try:
   692	+            if self.log_handle:
   693	+                self.log_handle.flush()
   694	+            return self.server_log_path.stat().st_size
   695	+        except OSError:
   696	+            return 0
   697	+
   698	+    def log_delta_from(self, offset):
   699	+        try:
   700	+            if self.log_handle:
   701	+                self.log_handle.flush()
   702	+            with open(self.server_log_path, 'rb') as f:
   703	+                f.seek(offset)
   704	+                return f.read().decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
   705	+        except OSError:
   706	+            return ''
   707	+
   708	+    def log_tail(self, n_lines=25):
   709	+        try:
   710	+            if self.log_handle:
   711	+                self.log_handle.flush()
   712	+            lines = self.server_log_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').splitlines()
   713	+            return '\n'.join(lines[-n_lines:])
   714	+        except OSError:
   715	+            return ''
   716	+
   717	+    def stop(self):
   718	+        self.sample_vmhwm()
   719	+        if self.proc is not None:
   720	+            if self.proc.poll() is None:
   721	+                self.proc.terminate()
   722	+                try:
   723	+                    self.proc.wait(timeout=10)
   724	+                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   725	+                    self.proc.kill()
   726	+                    self.proc.wait(timeout=10)
   727	+            self.exit_code = self.proc.returncode
   728	+        if self.log_handle is not None:
   729	+            try:
   730	+                self.log_handle.flush()
   731	+                self.log_handle.close()
   732	+            except OSError:
   733	+                pass
   734	+            self.log_handle = None
   735	+        self.ended_utc = utc_now()
   736	+
   737	+    def summary(self):
   738	+        return {
   739	+            'label': self.label,
   740	+            'budget': self.budget,
   741	+            'command': self.cmd,
   742	+            'started_utc': self.started_utc,
   743	+            'ended_utc': self.ended_utc,
   744	+            'startup_s_not_a_measurement': self.startup_s_not_a_measurement,
   745	+            'server_pid': self.server_pid,
   746	+            'server_name': self.server_name,
   747	+            'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': self.peak_vmhwm_kib,
   748	+            'exit_code': self.exit_code,
   749	+            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   750	+        }
   751	+
   752	+
   753	+def run_step_a_template_check(target_frames, post_fn=http_json, get_fn=http_json, sample_hwm_fn=None):
   754	+    """Run Step A (template check + text A/B on the target frames) against the server on 8090."""
   755	+    props = get_fn('/props')
   756	+    chat_template = props.get('chat_template', '')
   757	+    dgs = props.get('default_generation_settings') or {}
   758	+    props_summary = {
   759	+        'model_alias': props.get('model_alias'),
   760	+        'model_path': props.get('model_path'),
   761	+        'model_ftype': props.get('model_ftype'),
   762	+        'total_slots': props.get('total_slots'),
   763	+        'n_ctx': dgs.get('n_ctx'),
   764	+        'build_info': props.get('build_info'),
   765	+        'bos_token': props.get('bos_token'),
   766	+        'eos_token': props.get('eos_token'),
   767	+        'modalities': props.get('modalities'),
   768	+    }
   769	+
   770	+    tokenize_results = []
   771	+    for marker in TOKENIZE_MARKERS:
   772	+        res = post_fn('/tokenize', build_tokenize_payload(marker))
   773	+        tokens = res.get('tokens') or []
   774	+        tokenize_results.append(classify_tokenized_marker(marker, tokens))
   775	+
   776	+    apply_res = post_fn('/apply-template', build_apply_template_payload(APPLY_TEMPLATE_MESSAGES))
   777	+    rendered_hi = apply_res.get('prompt', '')
   778	+
   779	+    verdict = evaluate_template_verdict(chat_template, tokenize_results, rendered_hi)
   780	+
   781	+    text_ab = []
   782	+    for item in target_frames:
   783	+        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   784	+        old_raw = post_fn('/completion', build_old_completion_payload(item['old_prompt']))
   785	+        old_wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
   786	+        old_parsed = extract_completion_reply(old_raw)
   787	+        old_parsed['wall_ms_not_a_measurement'] = old_wall_ms
   788	+        if sample_hwm_fn:
   789	+            sample_hwm_fn()
   790	+
   791	+        t1 = time.perf_counter()
   792	+        new_raw = post_fn('/chat/completions', build_new_chat_text_payload(item['user_text']))
   793	+        new_wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t1) * 1000.0
   794	+        new_parsed = extract_chat_reply(new_raw)
   795	+        new_parsed['wall_ms_not_a_measurement'] = new_wall_ms
   796	+        if sample_hwm_fn:
   797	+            sample_hwm_fn()
   798	+
   799	+        text_ab.append({
   800	+            'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
   801	+            'photo': item['photo'],
   802	+            'yolo_lines': item['yolo_lines'],
   803	+            'old_completion': old_parsed,
   804	+            'new_chat_completions': new_parsed,
   805	+            'm2q1_saved_replies': item.get('m2q1_replies'),
   806	+            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   807	+        })
   808	+
   809	+    return {
   810	+        'verdict': verdict,
   811	+        'verdict_statement': (
   812	+            f'{verdict}: Gemma 3 markers (<start_of_turn>, <end_of_turn>) are tokenized as multiple '
   813	+            'plain-text pieces, whereas Gemma 4 markers (<|turn>, <turn|>) are single special tokens '
   814	+            'and are used by the GGUF chat_template.'
   815	+            if verdict == 'CONFIRMED'
   816	+            else f'{verdict}: see tokenize_checks and rendered_hi.'
   817	+        ),
   818	+        'props_summary': props_summary,
   819	+        'chat_template': chat_template,
   820	+        'tokenize_checks': tokenize_results,
   821	+        'apply_template_hi': {
   822	+            'messages': APPLY_TEMPLATE_MESSAGES,
   823	+            'rendered_prompt': rendered_hi,
   824	+        },
   825	+        'text_ab': text_ab,
   826	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   827	+    }
   828	+
   829	+
   830	+def run_vision_budget_pass(
   831	+    budget,
   832	+    target_frames,
   833	+    server_factory,
   834	+    post_fn=http_json,
   835	+    with_yolo=False,
   836	+    log_fn=print,
   837	+):
   838	+    """Run one vision budget pass across target_frames with crash handling (restart at most once, stop on 2 consecutive crashes)."""
   839	+    label = f'vision_budget_{budget}_yolo' if with_yolo else f'vision_budget_{budget}'
   840	+    server = server_factory(budget=budget, label=label)
   841	+    server_summaries = []
   842	+    calls = []
   843	+    crashes = 0
   844	+    consecutive_crashes = 0
   845	+    restarted_once = False
   846	+    stopped_early = False
   847	+    stop_reason = None
   848	+
   849	+    try:
   850	+        server.start()
   851	+    except Exception as e:
   852	+        server.stop()
   853	+        server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   854	+        return {
   855	+            'budget': budget,
   856	+            'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   857	+            'optional': bool(with_yolo),
   858	+            'crashes': 1,
   859	+            'stopped_early': True,
   860	+            'stop_reason': f'startup_failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}',
   861	+            'servers': server_summaries,
   862	+            'calls': [],
   863	+            'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   864	+        }
   865	+
   866	+    try:
   867	+        for idx, item in enumerate(target_frames):
   868	+            img_bytes = Path(item['frame_path']).read_bytes()
   869	+            prompt_text = (
   870	+                build_optional_yolo_vision_text(item['yolo_lines'])
   871	+                if with_yolo
   872	+                else VISION_PROMPT
   873	+            )
   874	+            payload = build_vision_chat_payload(img_bytes, text_prompt=prompt_text)
   875	+            offset = server.log_size()
   876	+            t0 = time.perf_counter()
   877	+            try:
   878	+                resp = post_fn('/chat/completions', payload)
   879	+                wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
   880	+                server.sample_vmhwm()
   881	+                delta = server.log_delta_from(offset)
   882	+                log_info = parse_server_log_delta(delta)
   883	+                parsed = extract_chat_reply(resp)
   884	+                call_rec = {
   885	+                    'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
   886	+                    'photo': item['photo'],
   887	+                    'budget': budget,
   888	+                    'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   889	+                    'status': 'ok',
   890	+                    'crashed': False,
   891	+                    'reply': parsed['reply'],
   892	+                    'raw_content': parsed['raw_content'],
   893	+                    'reasoning_content': parsed['reasoning_content'],
   894	+                    'finish_reason': parsed['finish_reason'],
   895	+                    'prompt_tokens': parsed['prompt_tokens'],
   896	+                    'completion_tokens': parsed['completion_tokens'],
   897	+                    'image_tokens_logged': log_info['image_tokens_logged'],
   898	+                    'image_tokens_batches': log_info['image_tokens_batches'],
   899	+                    'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': parsed['prompt_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   900	+                    'predicted_ms_not_a_measurement': parsed['predicted_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   901	+                    'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': parsed['predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement'],
   902	+                    'wall_ms_not_a_measurement': wall_ms,
   903	+                    'image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement': log_info['image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   904	+                    'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   905	+                }
   906	+                calls.append(call_rec)
   907	+                consecutive_crashes = 0
   908	+                log_fn(
   909	+                    f"[{label}] {item['frame_stem']}: ok, img_tok={log_info['image_tokens_logged']}, "
   910	+                    f"prompt_tok={parsed['prompt_tokens']}, wall_ms={wall_ms:.1f} ({TIMING_LABEL})"
   911	+                )
   912	+            except Exception as e:
   913	+                wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
   914	+                time.sleep(0.2)
   915	+                alive = server.is_alive()
   916	+                delta = server.log_delta_from(offset)
   917	+                log_info = parse_server_log_delta(delta)
   918	+                tail = server.log_tail(30)
   919	+                is_http_error = isinstance(e, urllib.error.HTTPError)
   920	+                is_crash = (not alive) or (
   921	+                    not is_http_error
   922	+                    and isinstance(e, (http.client.RemoteDisconnected, ConnectionResetError, urllib.error.URLError))
   923	+                )
   924	+                if is_crash:
   925	+                    crashes += 1
   926	+                    consecutive_crashes += 1
   927	+                call_rec = {
   928	+                    'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
   929	+                    'photo': item['photo'],
   930	+                    'budget': budget,
   931	+                    'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   932	+                    'status': 'crash' if is_crash else 'error',
   933	+                    'crashed': is_crash,
   934	+                    'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}',
   935	+                    'server_exit_code': server.proc.poll() if server.proc is not None else None,
   936	+                    'reply': None,
   937	+                    'prompt_tokens': None,
   938	+                    'image_tokens_logged': log_info['image_tokens_logged'],
   939	+                    'prompt_ms_not_a_measurement': None,
   940	+                    'predicted_per_second_not_a_measurement': None,
   941	+                    'wall_ms_not_a_measurement': wall_ms,
   942	+                    'image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement': log_info['image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement'],
   943	+                    'log_tail': tail,
   944	+                    'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   945	+                }
   946	+                calls.append(call_rec)
   947	+                log_fn(f"[{label}] {item['frame_stem']}: {'CRASH' if is_crash else 'ERROR'} ({e})")
   948	+
   949	+                if is_crash:
   950	+                    server.stop()
   951	+                    server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   952	+                    if consecutive_crashes >= 2:
   953	+                        stopped_early = True
   954	+                        stop_reason = 'two_consecutive_crashes_at_budget'
   955	+                        break
   956	+                    if restarted_once:
   957	+                        stopped_early = True
   958	+                        stop_reason = 'already_restarted_once_at_budget'
   959	+                        break
   960	+                    if idx + 1 < len(target_frames):
   961	+                        restarted_once = True
   962	+                        server = server_factory(budget=budget, label=f'{label}_restart1')
   963	+                        try:
   964	+                            server.start()
   965	+                        except Exception as re_err:
   966	+                            crashes += 1
   967	+                            consecutive_crashes += 1
   968	+                            server.stop()
   969	+                            server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   970	+                            stopped_early = True
   971	+                            stop_reason = f'restart_failed: {type(re_err).__name__}: {re_err}'
   972	+                            break
   973	+    finally:
   974	+        if server.proc is not None and (not server_summaries or server_summaries[-1]['started_utc'] != server.started_utc):
   975	+            server.stop()
   976	+            server_summaries.append(server.summary())
   977	+
   978	+    return {
   979	+        'budget': budget,
   980	+        'with_yolo': with_yolo,
   981	+        'optional': bool(with_yolo),
   982	+        'crashes': crashes,
   983	+        'stopped_early': stopped_early,
   984	+        'stop_reason': stop_reason,
   985	+        'servers': server_summaries,
   986	+        'calls': calls,
   987	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
   988	+    }
   989	+
   990	+
   991	+def _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, x, y, lines, width_chars=100, font_size=13, line_gap=4, fill='#111111'):
   992	+    font = ImageFont.load_default(size=font_size)
   993	+    for raw_line in lines:
   994	+        text = str(raw_line)
   995	+        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(text, width=width_chars) if text else ['']
   996	+        for part in wrapped:
   997	+            draw.text((x, y), part, fill=fill, font=font)
   998	+            y += font_size + line_gap
   999	+    return y
  1000	+
  1001	+
  1002	+def build_vis0_sheet_pdf(results_data, target_frames, pdf_path):
  1003	+    """Build vis0_sheet.pdf (PIL only, < 20 MB): Page 1 = template check; then 1 page per frame."""
  1004	+    page_w, page_h = 960, 1480
  1005	+    total_pages = 1 + len(target_frames)
  1006	+    pages = []
  1007	+
  1008	+    # Page 1: Template Check Results
  1009	+    p1 = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
  1010	+    draw1 = ImageDraw.Draw(p1)
  1011	+    draw1.text(
  1012	+        (24, 18),
  1013	+        f'VIS-0 Template Check & Vision Feasibility  --  Page 1 / {total_pages}',
  1014	+        fill='#000000',
  1015	+        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=18),
  1016	+    )
  1017	+    y = 48
  1018	+    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(
  1019	+        draw1, 24, y, [f'Labels: {REPORT_LABELS}'], width_chars=106, font_size=12, fill='#333333'
  1020	+    )
  1021	+    y += 10
  1022	+
  1023	+    tc = results_data['template_check']
  1024	+    draw1.text(
  1025	+        (24, y),
  1026	+        f"Step A Verdict: {tc['verdict']}",
  1027	+        fill='#000000',
  1028	+        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=16),
  1029	+    )
  1030	+    y += 24
  1031	+    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, [tc['verdict_statement']], width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1032	+    y += 12
  1033	+
  1034	+    draw1.text(
  1035	+        (24, y),
  1036	+        '1. /tokenize with "with_pieces": true:',
  1037	+        fill='#000000',
  1038	+        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1039	+    )
  1040	+    y += 22
  1041	+    tok_lines = []
  1042	+    for t_row in tc['tokenize_checks']:
  1043	+        pieces_str = ', '.join(
  1044	+            f"{t['id']}:{t['piece']!r}" for t in t_row['tokens']
  1045	+        )
  1046	+        tok_lines.append(
  1047	+            f"- {t_row['input']!r} -> {t_row['classification']}  [{pieces_str}]"
  1048	+        )
  1049	+    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, tok_lines, width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1050	+    y += 12
  1051	+
  1052	+    draw1.text(
  1053	+        (24, y),
  1054	+        '2. /apply-template with [{"role": "user", "content": "Hi"}] (verbatim repr):',
  1055	+        fill='#000000',
  1056	+        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1057	+    )
  1058	+    y += 22
  1059	+    rendered_hi = tc['apply_template_hi']['rendered_prompt']
  1060	+    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, [repr(rendered_hi)], width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1061	+    y += 12
  1062	+
  1063	+    draw1.text(
  1064	+        (24, y),
  1065	+        '3. /props chat_template header & turn-token lines:',
  1066	+        fill='#000000',
  1067	+        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1068	+    )
  1069	+    y += 22
  1070	+    ct_lines = tc['chat_template'].splitlines()
  1071	+    ct_excerpt = ct_lines[:6] + ['...'] + [
  1072	+        ln.strip() for ln in ct_lines if '<|turn>' in ln or '<turn|>' in ln
  1073	+    ][:8]
  1074	+    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, ct_excerpt, width_chars=102, font_size=12, fill='#222222')
  1075	+    y += 12
  1076	+
  1077	+    draw1.text(
  1078	+        (24, y),
  1079	+        '4. Vision Budgets Summary (NOT measurements; agent resident):',
  1080	+        fill='#000000',
  1081	+        font=ImageFont.load_default(size=15),
  1082	+    )
  1083	+    y += 22
  1084	+    vis_summary_lines = []
  1085	+    for b_key, b_pass in results_data.get('vision_budgets', {}).items():
  1086	+        ok_calls = [c for c in b_pass['calls'] if c['status'] == 'ok']
  1087	+        img_toks = sorted({c['image_tokens_logged'] for c in ok_calls if c.get('image_tokens_logged') is not None})
  1088	+        vis_summary_lines.append(
  1089	+            f"- Budget {b_key}: {len(ok_calls)}/{len(b_pass['calls'])} ok, "
  1090	+            f"crashes={b_pass['crashes']}, logged_image_tokens={img_toks}"
  1091	+        )
  1092	+    opt_pass = results_data.get('optional_140_yolo')
  1093	+    if opt_pass:
  1094	+        ok_opt = [c for c in opt_pass['calls'] if c['status'] == 'ok']
  1095	+        vis_summary_lines.append(
  1096	+            f"- Optional 140+YOLO: {len(ok_opt)}/{len(opt_pass['calls'])} ok, crashes={opt_pass['crashes']}"
  1097	+        )
  1098	+    y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw1, 32, y, vis_summary_lines, width_chars=102, font_size=13)
  1099	+    pages.append(p1)
  1100	+
  1101	+    # Index lookup for per-frame pages
  1102	+    text_by_stem = {r['frame_stem']: r for r in tc['text_ab']}
  1103	+    vis_by_budget_stem = {}
  1104	+    for b_key, b_pass in results_data.get('vision_budgets', {}).items():
  1105	+        vis_by_budget_stem[str(b_key)] = {c['frame_stem']: c for c in b_pass['calls']}
  1106	+    opt_by_stem = {}
  1107	+    if opt_pass:
  1108	+        opt_by_stem = {c['frame_stem']: c for c in opt_pass['calls']}
  1109	+
  1110	+    for idx, item in enumerate(target_frames, 2):
  1111	+        stem = item['frame_stem']
  1112	+        page = Image.new('RGB', (page_w, page_h), 'white')
  1113	+        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
  1114	+        draw.text(
  1115	+            (24, 16),
  1116	+            f"{stem} ({item['photo']})  --  Page {idx} / {total_pages}",
  1117	+            fill='#000000',
  1118	+            font=ImageFont.load_default(size=17),
  1119	+        )
  1120	+
  1121	+        with Image.open(item['frame_path']) as raw_img:
  1122	+            disp_img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(raw_img).convert('RGB')
  1123	+        disp_img.thumbnail((560, 420), Image.Resampling.BILINEAR)
  1124	+        img_x = (page_w - disp_img.width) // 2
  1125	+        img_y = 44
  1126	+        page.paste(disp_img, (img_x, img_y))
  1127	+        y = img_y + disp_img.height + 12
  1128	+
  1129	+        draw.text((24, y), 'YOLO detections (Y-ONLY input lines):', fill='#000000', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=14))
  1130	+        y += 18
  1131	+        yolo_preview = item['yolo_lines'].splitlines()
  1132	+        if len(yolo_preview) > 6:
  1133	+            yolo_preview = ['; '.join(yolo_preview[:6]), '; '.join(yolo_preview[6:])]
  1134	+        else:
  1135	+            yolo_preview = ['; '.join(yolo_preview)] if yolo_preview else ['(none)']
  1136	+        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, yolo_preview, width_chars=106, font_size=12, fill='#333333')
  1137	+        y += 8
  1138	+
  1139	+        t_rec = text_by_stem.get(stem, {})
  1140	+        old_c = t_rec.get('old_completion', {})
  1141	+        new_c = t_rec.get('new_chat_completions', {})
  1142	+
  1143	+        draw.text(
  1144	+            (24, y),
  1145	+            f"Text OLD (/completion, Gemma 3 markers) [prompt_tok={old_c.get('tokens_evaluated')}]:",
  1146	+            fill='#8b0000',
  1147	+            font=ImageFont.load_default(size=14),
  1148	+        )
  1149	+        y += 18
  1150	+        old_text = old_c.get('raw_content', old_c.get('reply', ''))
  1151	+        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(
  1152	+            draw, 32, y, [repr(old_text) if ('<' in str(old_text) or '\n' in str(old_text)) else str(old_text)],
  1153	+            width_chars=104, font_size=13,
  1154	+        )
  1155	+        y += 8
  1156	+
  1157	+        draw.text(
  1158	+            (24, y),
  1159	+            f"Text NEW (/chat/completions, GGUF template) [prompt_tok={new_c.get('prompt_tokens')}]:",
  1160	+            fill='#004d00',
  1161	+            font=ImageFont.load_default(size=14),
  1162	+        )
  1163	+        y += 18
  1164	+        new_reply_text = new_c.get('reply') or ''
  1165	+        if not new_reply_text and new_c.get('reasoning_content'):
  1166	+            new_reply_text = f"[empty content; reasoning_content]: {new_c['reasoning_content']}"
  1167	+        y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, new_reply_text.splitlines() or ['(empty reply)'], width_chars=104, font_size=13)
  1168	+        y += 10
  1169	+
  1170	+        for b in VISION_BUDGETS:
  1171	+            b_str = str(b)
  1172	+            v_call = vis_by_budget_stem.get(b_str, {}).get(stem)
  1173	+            if v_call is None:
  1174	+                hdr = f'Vision budget {b}: (not run — budget stopped early)'
  1175	+                body_lines = ['(not run)']
  1176	+            elif v_call['status'] != 'ok':
  1177	+                hdr = f"Vision budget {b}: {v_call['status'].upper()} ({v_call.get('error', '')})"
  1178	+                body_lines = (v_call.get('log_tail') or '').splitlines()[-3:] or ['(no output)']
  1179	+            else:
  1180	+                hdr = (
  1181	+                    f"Vision budget {b} [prompt_tokens={v_call.get('prompt_tokens')}, "
  1182	+                    f"image_tokens_logged={v_call.get('image_tokens_logged')}]:"
  1183	+                )
  1184	+                v_txt = v_call.get('reply') or ''
  1185	+                if not v_txt and v_call.get('reasoning_content'):
  1186	+                    v_txt = f"[empty content; reasoning_content]: {v_call['reasoning_content']}"
  1187	+                body_lines = v_txt.splitlines() or ['(empty reply)']
  1188	+            draw.text((24, y), hdr, fill='#003366', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=14))
  1189	+            y += 18
  1190	+            y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, body_lines, width_chars=104, font_size=13)
  1191	+            y += 8
  1192	+
  1193	+        if stem in opt_by_stem:
  1194	+            o_call = opt_by_stem[stem]
  1195	+            hdr = (
  1196	+                f"Optional Vision 140 + YOLO [prompt_tokens={o_call.get('prompt_tokens')}, "
  1197	+                f"image_tokens_logged={o_call.get('image_tokens_logged')}]:"
  1198	+            )
  1199	+            o_txt = o_call.get('reply') or ''
  1200	+            if not o_txt and o_call.get('reasoning_content'):
  1201	+                o_txt = f"[empty content; reasoning_content]: {o_call['reasoning_content']}"
  1202	+            draw.text((24, y), hdr, fill='#4b0082', font=ImageFont.load_default(size=14))
  1203	+            y += 18
  1204	+            y = _draw_wrapped_lines(draw, 32, y, o_txt.splitlines() or ['(empty reply)'], width_chars=104, font_size=13)
  1205	+
  1206	+        pages.append(page)
  1207	+
  1208	+    pdf_path = Path(pdf_path)
  1209	+    pdf_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1210	+    pages[0].save(pdf_path, format='PDF', resolution=100.0, save_all=True, append_images=pages[1:])
  1211	+    if pdf_path.stat().st_size >= 20 * 1024 * 1024:
  1212	+        raise RuntimeError(f'{pdf_path} exceeds 20 MB limit')
