  1214	+
  1215	+def git_base_commit(root=ROBOT):
  1216	+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip()
  1217	+
  1218	+
  1219	+RUN_OUTPUT_FILES = ('results.json', 'manifest.json', 'server.log', 'run.log', 'vis0_sheet.pdf')
  1220	+
  1221	+
  1222	+def resolve_and_validate_out_dir(out_dir=None, runs_root=None):
  1223	+    """Resolve out_dir under benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/runs/ and reject outside paths or existing outputs."""
  1224	+    allowed_root = Path(HERE / 'runs' if runs_root is None else runs_root).resolve()
  1225	+    if out_dir is None:
  1226	+        stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
  1227	+        resolved = (allowed_root / stamp).resolve()
  1228	+    else:
  1229	+        resolved = Path(out_dir).resolve()
  1230	+    if resolved == allowed_root or not resolved.is_relative_to(allowed_root):
  1231	+        raise ValueError(
  1232	+            f'--out must resolve to a subdirectory inside {allowed_root}, got {resolved}'
  1233	+        )
  1234	+    for fname in RUN_OUTPUT_FILES:
  1235	+        existing = resolved / fname
  1236	+        if existing.exists():
  1237	+            raise FileExistsError(f'Refusing to overwrite existing output artifact: {existing}')
  1238	+    return resolved
  1239	+
  1240	+
  1241	+def run_harness(limit=None, out_dir=None, skip_optional=False):
  1242	+    start_utc = utc_now()
  1243	+    t0 = time.perf_counter()
  1244	+    out_dir = resolve_and_validate_out_dir(out_dir=out_dir)
  1245	+    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1246	+    run_log_path = out_dir / 'run.log'
  1247	+    server_log_path = out_dir / 'server.log'
  1248	+
  1249	+    def log(msg):
  1250	+        line = f'[{utc_now()}] {msg}'
  1251	+        print(line, flush=True)
  1252	+        with open(run_log_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as lf:
  1253	+            lf.write(line + '\n')
  1254	+
  1255	+    log(f'START VIS-0 harness ({REPORT_LABELS})')
  1256	+    check_port_8080_stopped(ROBOT_PORT)
  1257	+
  1258	+    for req_file, exp_bytes, exp_sha in (
  1259	+        (MODEL_GGUF, MODEL_EXPECTED_BYTES, MODEL_EXPECTED_SHA256),
  1260	+        (MMPROJ_GGUF, MMPROJ_EXPECTED_BYTES, MMPROJ_EXPECTED_SHA256),
  1261	+    ):
  1262	+        if not req_file.is_file():
  1263	+            raise RuntimeError(f'Missing required weight file: {req_file}')
  1264	+        actual_bytes = req_file.stat().st_size
  1265	+        actual_sha = sha256_file(req_file)
  1266	+        if actual_bytes != exp_bytes or actual_sha != exp_sha:
  1267	+            raise RuntimeError(
  1268	+                f'Weight verification failed for {req_file}: '
  1269	+                f'bytes={actual_bytes} (expected {exp_bytes}), sha256={actual_sha} (expected {exp_sha})'
  1270	+            )
  1271	+
  1272	+    target_frames = load_target_frames(limit=limit)
  1273	+    log(f'Loaded {len(target_frames)} target frames from {M2Q1_RUN_DIR} (limit={limit})')
  1274	+
  1275	+    all_server_summaries = []
  1276	+
  1277	+    # Step A runs on the first server instance (started with budget 70), then we run Step B budget 70 on it,
  1278	+    # or we run Step A on a dedicated budget=70 server and then run budgets 70, 140, 280 cleanly.
  1279	+    # Starting one server for Step A (with --image-min-tokens 70 --image-max-tokens 70) and stopping it cleanly
  1280	+    # before the Step B budget loop keeps Step A and each Step B budget completely isolated.
  1281	+    step_a_server = ManagedTestServer(budget=VISION_BUDGETS[0], server_log_path=server_log_path, label='step_a_template_check')
  1282	+    try:
  1283	+        step_a_server.start()
  1284	+        log(f'Step A server ready on {TEST_URL} (pid={step_a_server.server_pid})')
  1285	+        template_check = run_step_a_template_check(
  1286	+            target_frames,
  1287	+            post_fn=http_json,
  1288	+            get_fn=http_json,
  1289	+            sample_hwm_fn=step_a_server.sample_vmhwm,
  1290	+        )
  1291	+        log(f"Step A complete: verdict={template_check['verdict']}")
  1292	+    finally:
  1293	+        step_a_server.stop()
  1294	+        all_server_summaries.append(step_a_server.summary())
  1295	+
  1296	+    def server_factory(budget, label):
  1297	+        return ManagedTestServer(budget=budget, server_log_path=server_log_path, label=label)
  1298	+
  1299	+    vision_budgets = {}
  1300	+    for budget in VISION_BUDGETS:
  1301	+        log(f'Starting Step B vision pass for budget={budget}')
  1302	+        b_res = run_vision_budget_pass(
  1303	+            budget=budget,
  1304	+            target_frames=target_frames,
  1305	+            server_factory=server_factory,
  1306	+            post_fn=http_json,
  1307	+            with_yolo=False,
  1308	+            log_fn=log,
  1309	+        )
  1310	+        vision_budgets[str(budget)] = b_res
  1311	+        all_server_summaries.extend(b_res['servers'])
  1312	+
  1313	+    all_budgets_ok = all(
  1314	+        b_res['crashes'] == 0
  1315	+        and not b_res['stopped_early']
  1316	+        and len(b_res['calls']) == len(target_frames)
  1317	+        and all(c['status'] == 'ok' for c in b_res['calls'])
  1318	+        for b_res in vision_budgets.values()
  1319	+    )
  1320	+
  1321	+    optional_140_yolo = None
  1322	+    if all_budgets_ok and not skip_optional:
  1323	+        log('All budgets (70, 140, 280) succeeded without crash; running optional 140 + YOLO pass')
  1324	+        optional_140_yolo = run_vision_budget_pass(
  1325	+            budget=140,
  1326	+            target_frames=target_frames,
  1327	+            server_factory=server_factory,
  1328	+            post_fn=http_json,
  1329	+            with_yolo=True,
  1330	+            log_fn=log,
  1331	+        )
  1332	+        all_server_summaries.extend(optional_140_yolo['servers'])
  1333	+
  1334	+    peak_vmhwm_kib = max(
  1335	+        (s['peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement'] for s in all_server_summaries if s['peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement'] is not None),
  1336	+        default=None,
  1337	+    )
  1338	+
  1339	+    results_payload = {
  1340	+        'task': 'VIS-0',
  1341	+        'labels': REPORT_LABELS,
  1342	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
  1343	+        'limit': limit,
  1344	+        'template_check': template_check,
  1345	+        'vision_budgets': vision_budgets,
  1346	+        'optional_140_yolo': optional_140_yolo,
  1347	+        'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': peak_vmhwm_kib,
  1348	+        'servers': all_server_summaries,
  1349	+    }
  1350	+    write_json(out_dir / 'results.json', results_payload)
  1351	+
  1352	+    pdf_path = out_dir / 'vis0_sheet.pdf'
  1353	+    build_vis0_sheet_pdf(results_payload, target_frames, pdf_path)
  1354	+    DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1355	+    shutil.copy2(pdf_path, DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR / 'vis0_sheet.pdf')
  1356	+    shutil.copy2(pdf_path, DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR / f'vis0_sheet_{out_dir.name}.pdf')
  1357	+    log(f'Built {pdf_path.name} ({pdf_path.stat().st_size} B) and copied to {DOWNLOAD_PDF_DIR}')
  1358	+
  1359	+    end_utc = utc_now()
  1360	+    elapsed_s = time.perf_counter() - t0
  1361	+    code_hashes = verify_and_hash_imports(ROBOT)
  1362	+
  1363	+    manifest = {
  1364	+        'task': 'VIS-0',
  1365	+        'labels': REPORT_LABELS,
  1366	+        'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
  1367	+        'base_commit': git_base_commit(ROBOT),
  1368	+        'start_utc': start_utc,
  1369	+        'end_utc': end_utc,
  1370	+        'elapsed_s_not_a_measurement': elapsed_s,
  1371	+        'limit': limit,
  1372	+        'code_sha256': code_hashes,
  1373	+        'build': {
  1374	+            **BUILD_INFO_RECORD,
  1375	+            'llama_server_bin': str(LLAMA_SERVER_BIN),
  1376	+            'llama_server_sha256': sha256_file(LLAMA_SERVER_BIN),
  1377	+            'llama_mtmd_cli_bin': str(LLAMA_MTMD_CLI_BIN),
  1378	+            'llama_mtmd_cli_sha256': sha256_file(LLAMA_MTMD_CLI_BIN),
  1379	+            'libggml_cpu_so': str(LIBGGML_CPU_SO),
  1380	+            'libggml_cpu_so_sha256': sha256_file(LIBGGML_CPU_SO),
  1381	+        },
  1382	+        'weights': {
  1383	+            'model_gguf': {
  1384	+                'path': str(MODEL_GGUF),
  1385	+                'source_repo': MODEL_SOURCE_REPO,
  1386	+                'source_revision': MODEL_SOURCE_REV,
  1387	+                'file_name': MODEL_GGUF.name,
  1388	+                'bytes': MODEL_EXPECTED_BYTES,
  1389	+                'sha256': MODEL_EXPECTED_SHA256,
  1390	+            },
  1391	+            'mmproj_gguf': {
  1392	+                'path': str(MMPROJ_GGUF),
  1393	+                'source_repo': MMPROJ_SOURCE_REPO,
  1394	+                'source_revision': MMPROJ_SOURCE_REV,
  1395	+                'file_name': MMPROJ_GGUF.name,
  1396	+                'bytes': MMPROJ_EXPECTED_BYTES,
  1397	+                'sha256': MMPROJ_EXPECTED_SHA256,
  1398	+            },
  1399	+        },
  1400	+        'server_commands': {
  1401	+            str(b): build_server_cmd(budget=b) for b in VISION_BUDGETS
  1402	+        },
  1403	+        'server_props': template_check['props_summary'],
  1404	+        'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': peak_vmhwm_kib,
  1405	+        'interpreter': {
  1406	+            'executable': sys.executable,
  1407	+            'python_version': sys.version.split()[0],
  1408	+            'platform': sys.platform,
  1409	+            'pil_version': Image.__version__,
  1410	+        },
  1411	+        'frames': [
  1412	+            {
  1413	+                'requested_stem': item['requested_stem'],
  1414	+                'frame_stem': item['frame_stem'],
  1415	+                'match_note': item['match_note'],
  1416	+                'photo': item['photo'],
  1417	+                'frame_path': item['frame_path'],
  1418	+                'frame_sha256': item['frame_sha256'],
  1419	+                'frame_bytes': item['frame_bytes'],
  1420	+                'frame_size': item['frame_size'],
  1421	+                'detection_count': item['detection_count'],
  1422	+            }
  1423	+            for item in target_frames
  1424	+        ],
  1425	+    }
  1426	+    write_json(out_dir / 'manifest.json', manifest)
  1427	+    log(f'DONE VIS-0 harness in {elapsed_s:.2f}s ({TIMING_LABEL}) -> {out_dir}')
  1428	+    return out_dir
  1429	+
  1430	+
  1431	+def main(argv=None):
  1432	+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  1433	+    parser.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=None, help='Process only the first N frames (smoke run)')
  1434	+    parser.add_argument('--out', type=Path, default=None, help='Output directory (default: runs/<UTC>)')
  1435	+    parser.add_argument('--skip-optional', action='store_true', help='Skip optional 140+YOLO pass')
  1436	+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
  1437	+    if args.limit is not None and args.limit < 1:
  1438	+        parser.error('--limit must be >= 1')
  1439	+    run_harness(limit=args.limit, out_dir=args.out, skip_optional=args.skip_optional)
  1440	+
  1441	+
  1442	+if __name__ == '__main__':
  1443	+    main()

  1471	+class FakeServer:
  1472	+    def __init__(self, budget, label, fail_start=False, alive_sequence=None, log_text=''):
  1473	+        self.budget = budget
  1474	+        self.label = label
  1475	+        self.fail_start = fail_start
  1476	+        self.alive_sequence = list(alive_sequence or [True])
  1477	+        self.log_text = log_text
  1478	+        self.proc = type('FakeProc', (), {'pid': 99999, 'poll': lambda self_: None if self.is_alive() else 139})()
  1479	+        self.started_utc = f'2026-10-18T00:00:00Z_{label}'
  1480	+        self.ended_utc = None
  1481	+        self.stopped = False
  1482	+
  1483	+    def start(self):
  1484	+        if self.fail_start:
  1485	+            raise RuntimeError('simulated start failure')
  1486	+
  1487	+    def stop(self):
  1488	+        self.stopped = True
  1489	+        self.ended_utc = f'2026-10-18T00:01:00Z_{self.label}'
  1490	+
  1491	+    def is_alive(self):
  1492	+        if len(self.alive_sequence) > 1:
  1493	+            return self.alive_sequence.pop(0)
  1494	+        return self.alive_sequence[0]
  1495	+
  1496	+    def sample_vmhwm(self):
  1497	+        return 2900000
  1498	+
  1499	+    def log_size(self):
  1500	+        return 0
  1501	+
  1502	+    def log_delta_from(self, offset):
  1503	+        return self.log_text[offset:]
  1504	+
  1505	+    def log_tail(self, n_lines=25):
  1506	+        return '\n'.join(self.log_text.splitlines()[-n_lines:])
  1507	+
  1508	+    def summary(self):
  1509	+        return {
  1510	+            'label': self.label,
  1511	+            'budget': self.budget,
  1512	+            'command': vis0.build_server_cmd(budget=self.budget),
  1513	+            'started_utc': self.started_utc,
  1514	+            'ended_utc': self.ended_utc,
  1515	+            'startup_s_not_a_measurement': 1.0,
  1516	+            'server_pid': 99999,
  1517	+            'server_name': 'llama-server',
  1518	+            'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': 2900000,
  1519	+            'exit_code': 0 if self.is_alive() else 139,
  1520	+            'timing_label': vis0.TIMING_LABEL,
  1521	+        }

  1609	+    def test_classify_tokenized_marker_and_verdict(self):
  1610	+        sample = {
  1611	+            '<start_of_turn>user': [
  1612	+                {'id': 236820, 'piece': '<'},
  1613	+                {'id': 4159, 'piece': 'start'},
  1614	+                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
  1615	+                {'id': 1142, 'piece': 'of'},
  1616	+                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
  1617	+                {'id': 11438, 'piece': 'turn'},
  1618	+                {'id': 236813, 'piece': '>'},
  1619	+                {'id': 4186, 'piece': 'user'},
  1620	+            ],
  1621	+            '<end_of_turn>': [
  1622	+                {'id': 236820, 'piece': '<'},
  1623	+                {'id': 1711, 'piece': 'end'},
  1624	+                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
  1625	+                {'id': 1142, 'piece': 'of'},
  1626	+                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
  1627	+                {'id': 11438, 'piece': 'turn'},
  1628	+                {'id': 236813, 'piece': '>'},
  1629	+            ],
  1630	+            '<|turn>user': [
  1631	+                {'id': 105, 'piece': '<|turn>'},
  1632	+                {'id': 2364, 'piece': 'user'},
  1633	+            ],
  1634	+            '<turn|>': [
  1635	+                {'id': 106, 'piece': '<turn|>'},
  1636	+            ],
  1637	+            '<|turn>model': [
  1638	+                {'id': 105, 'piece': '<|turn>'},
  1639	+                {'id': 4368, 'piece': 'model'},
  1640	+            ],
  1641	+        }
  1642	+        checks = [vis0.classify_tokenized_marker(k, v) for k, v in sample.items()]
  1643	+        by_in = {c['input']: c for c in checks}
  1644	+        self.assertFalse(by_in['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
  1645	+        self.assertFalse(by_in['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
  1646	+        self.assertTrue(by_in['<|turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
  1647	+        self.assertTrue(by_in['<turn|>']['whole_input_is_single_token'])
  1648	+        self.assertTrue(by_in['<|turn>model']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
  1649	+
  1650	+        verdict = vis0.evaluate_template_verdict(
  1651	+            chat_template="{{ '<|turn>user\\n' + message['content'] + '<turn|>\\n<|turn>model\\n' }}",
  1652	+            tokenize_results=checks,
  1653	+            rendered_hi='<|bos|><|turn>user\nHi<turn|>\n<|turn>model\n<|thought\n<|channel|>',
  1654	+        )
  1655	+        self.assertEqual(verdict, 'CONFIRMED')

  1667	+    def test_vision_crash_restarts_at_most_once_and_stops_on_two_crashes(self):
  1668	+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
  1669	+            frames = []
  1670	+            for i in range(3):
  1671	+                p = Path(tmp) / f'f{i}.jpg'
  1672	+                Image.new('RGB', (32, 32), 'blue').save(p, format='JPEG')
  1673	+                frames.append({'frame_stem': f'f{i}', 'photo': f'f{i}.jpg', 'frame_path': str(p), 'yolo_lines': ''})
  1674	+
  1675	+            created = []
  1676	+
  1677	+            def factory(budget, label):
  1678	+                srv = FakeServer(budget=budget, label=label, alive_sequence=[False], log_text='SIGSEGV\n')
  1679	+                created.append(srv)
  1680	+                return srv
  1681	+
  1682	+            def crashing_post(path, payload):
  1683	+                raise ConnectionResetError('connection reset by peer')
  1684	+
  1685	+            res = vis0.run_vision_budget_pass(
  1686	+                budget=280,
  1687	+                target_frames=frames,
  1688	+                server_factory=factory,
  1689	+                post_fn=crashing_post,
  1690	+                log_fn=lambda _: None,
  1691	+            )
  1692	+            self.assertEqual(res['crashes'], 2)
  1693	+            self.assertTrue(res['stopped_early'])
  1694	+            self.assertEqual(res['stop_reason'], 'two_consecutive_crashes_at_budget')
  1695	+            self.assertEqual(len(res['calls']), 2)
  1696	+            self.assertEqual(len(created), 2)

  1760	+    def test_resolve_and_validate_out_dir_enforces_boundary_and_no_overwrite(self):
  1761	+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
  1762	+            runs_root = Path(tmp) / 'runs'
  1763	+            valid_dir = vis0.resolve_and_validate_out_dir(runs_root / '20261018T000000Z', runs_root=runs_root)
  1764	+            self.assertEqual(valid_dir, (runs_root / '20261018T000000Z').resolve())
  1765	+
  1766	+            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
  1767	+                vis0.resolve_and_validate_out_dir(Path(tmp) / 'outside', runs_root=runs_root)
  1768	+
  1769	+            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
  1770	+                vis0.resolve_and_validate_out_dir(runs_root, runs_root=runs_root)
  1771	+
  1772	+            valid_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1773	+            (valid_dir / 'results.json').write_text('{}', encoding='utf-8')
  1774	+            with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):
  1775	+                vis0.resolve_and_validate_out_dir(valid_dir, runs_root=runs_root)

  1777	+    def test_http_error_on_live_server_is_not_counted_as_crash(self):
  1778	+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
  1779	+            frames = []
  1780	+            for i in range(3):
  1781	+                p = Path(tmp) / f'f{i}.jpg'
  1782	+                Image.new('RGB', (32, 32), 'green').save(p, format='JPEG')
  1783	+                frames.append({'frame_stem': f'f{i}', 'photo': f'f{i}.jpg', 'frame_path': str(p), 'yolo_lines': ''})
  1784	+
  1785	+            created = []
  1786	+
  1787	+            def factory(budget, label):
  1788	+                srv = FakeServer(budget=budget, label=label, alive_sequence=[True], log_text='')
  1789	+                created.append(srv)
  1790	+                return srv
  1791	+
  1792	+            call_idx = {'n': 0}
  1793	+
  1794	+            def flaky_http_post(path, payload):
  1795	+                call_idx['n'] += 1
  1796	+                if call_idx['n'] <= 2:
  1797	+                    raise urllib.error.HTTPError(
  1798	+                        url='http://127.0.0.1:8090/chat/completions',
  1799	+                        code=400,
  1800	+                        msg='Bad Request',
  1801	+                        hdrs=None,
  1802	+                        fp=None,
  1803	+                    )
  1804	+                return {
  1805	+                    'choices': [{'message': {'content': 'Recovered on frame 2.'}, 'finish_reason': 'stop'}],
  1806	+                    'usage': {'prompt_tokens': 96, 'completion_tokens': 5},
  1807	+                    'timings': {'prompt_ms': 100.0, 'predicted_ms': 50.0, 'predicted_per_second': 10.0},
  1808	+                }
  1809	+
  1810	+            res = vis0.run_vision_budget_pass(
  1811	+                budget=70,
  1812	+                target_frames=frames,
  1813	+                server_factory=factory,
  1814	+                post_fn=flaky_http_post,
  1815	+                log_fn=lambda _: None,
  1816	+            )
  1817	+            self.assertEqual(res['crashes'], 0)
  1818	+            self.assertFalse(res['stopped_early'])
  1819	+            self.assertEqual(len(created), 1)
  1820	+            self.assertEqual([c['status'] for c in res['calls']], ['error', 'error', 'ok'])
  1821	+            self.assertEqual([c['crashed'] for c in res['calls']], [False, False, False])
  1822	+
  1823	+    def test_load_target_frames_validates_640px_dimensions_and_identity(self):

  1868	+                    results_json_path=results_path,

  1883	+    def test_check_port_8080_stopped_rejects_http_503_and_accepts_refused(self):
  1884	+        with unittest.mock.patch('urllib.request.urlopen') as mock_open, \
  1885	+                unittest.mock.patch.object(vis0, 'check_port_free') as mock_free:
  1886	+            mock_open.side_effect = urllib.error.HTTPError(
  1887	+                url='http://127.0.0.1:8080/health',
  1888	+                code=503,
  1889	+                msg='Service Unavailable',
  1890	+                hdrs=None,
  1891	+                fp=io.BytesIO(b'{"error":"loading model"}'),
  1892	+            )
  1893	+            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as ctx503:
  1894	+                vis0.check_port_8080_stopped(8080)
  1895	+            self.assertIn('503', str(ctx503.exception))
  1896	+            mock_free.assert_not_called()
  1897	+
  1898	+            mock_open.side_effect = urllib.error.URLError( TimeoutError('timed out') )
  1899	+            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as ctxtmo:
  1900	+                vis0.check_port_8080_stopped(8080)
  1901	+            self.assertIn('unexpected URLError', str(ctxtmo.exception))
  1902	+            mock_free.assert_not_called()
  1903	+
  1904	+            mock_open.side_effect = urllib.error.URLError(ConnectionRefusedError(111, 'Connection refused'))
  1905	+            self.assertTrue(vis0.check_port_8080_stopped(8080))
  1906	+            mock_free.assert_called_once_with(8080)
  1907	+
  1908	+
  1909	+if __name__ == '__main__':
  1910	+    unittest.main()

  1918	+# TASK VIS-0 — Gemma 4 Template Check & Gemma Vision Feasibility (Motors Off)
  1919	+
  1920	+**Labels:** Template check VALID (deterministic); vision replies VALID as outputs (temperature 0); timings and memory NOT measurements (agent resident); quality grading pending (Local AI)
  1921	+
  1922	+## Scope
  1923	+
  1924	+1. **Step A — Template Check (text only, port 8090):**
  1925	+   - Saves `GET /props` `chat_template` verbatim.
  1926	+   - Calls `POST /tokenize` with `"with_pieces": true` on `"<start_of_turn>user"`, `"<end_of_turn>"`, `"<|turn>user"`, `"<turn|>"`, and `"<|turn>model"`, recording whether each turn marker is `ONE special token` or `several plain-text pieces`.
  1927	+   - Calls `POST /apply-template` with `[{"role": "user", "content": "Hi"}]` and saves the rendered prompt verbatim.
  1928	+   - Runs a deterministic (`temperature: 0, top_k: 1, seed: 0, max 96 tokens, cache_prompt: false`) text A/B comparison across the 10 target frames using the saved `Y-ONLY` YOLO lines from `benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z/results.json`:
  1929	+     - **OLD:** `POST /completion` with the exact Gemma 3 marker string (`<start_of_turn>` / `<end_of_turn>`) constructed by `chat.build_prompt('gemma', ...)`.
  1930	+     - **NEW:** `POST /chat/completions` with the same instruction + YOLO lines as a single `user` message so `llama-server` applies the embedded GGUF template.
  1931	+   - Reports `CONFIRMED` / `REFUTED` / `UNCLEAR` on whether the Gemma 3 markers are wrong for `gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf`.
  1932	+
  1933	+2. **Step B — Vision Feasibility (port 8090):**
  1934	+   - Evaluates the 10 640 px frames from `benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z/frames/`:
  1935	+     `bench_photos__blocked_1`, `bench_photos__empty_2`, `bench_photos__person_facing_150cm`, `bench_photos__person_sideon_300cm`, `bench_photos__roomsig_couch_1`, `bench_photos__roomsig_refrigerator_1`, `bench_photos__roomsig_toilet_1`, `bench_photos__roomsig_toilet_2`, `test_photos__chiara_close_back`, `test_photos__abdel_close_right`.
  1936	+   - Sends `POST /chat/completions` with one user message containing the JPEG as a base64 `data:image/jpeg;base64,...` URI (`image_url`) plus:
  1937	+     `"Describe in one or two short sentences what is in front of the robot. Mention only what you can see."`
  1938	+   - Tests supported Gemma 4 image token budgets `70`, `140`, and `280` via `--image-min-tokens <N> --image-max-tokens <N>` on a separate `timeout 3600` server instance on `127.0.0.1:8090` (`--threads 4 --threads-batch 4 --parallel 1 --swa-full --cache-ram 0 --ctx-size 4096 --reasoning off -lv 4`, no CPU pinning).
  1939	+   - If all three budgets complete without crashing, runs an **optional** pass at budget `140` with `"Objects detected by YOLO:"` plus the saved `Y-ONLY` lines appended.
  1940	+
  1941	+## Build & Weights (Outside Repo)
  1942	+
  1943	+- **Separate `llama.cpp` build (`~/llama.cpp-vis`):**
  1944	+  - Source: `https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp` tag `b11716` (`797de63b2fa7396dcc8caca76834c699ac84e1c3`)
  1945	+  - Flags: `-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DGGML_NATIVE=OFF -DGGML_CPU_ARM_ARCH=armv8.2-a+dotprod+fp16 -DGGML_OPENCL=OFF`
  1946	+  - Compiler: Termux `Clang 21.1.8` (`aarch64-unknown-linux-android24`)
  1947	+  - `libggml-cpu.so` instruction counts: `sdot = 1117`, `fmla (8h) = 456`
  1948	+- **Model GGUF (`~/models/gemma-4-E2B-it-Q4_0.gguf`):**
  1949	+  - Source: `ggml-org/gemma-4-E2B-it-GGUF` (rev `b4243c156154b6dca9324415f8c7ccc098b4aed1`)
  1950	+  - Size: `2841481184` bytes
  1951	+  - SHA-256: `8e30dff3ac4c8434c49a7036fa15564bdbb6044e42bf04550bf1a096ad7e6a52`
  1952	+- **Vision Projector (`~/models/vis0/mmproj-gemma-4-E2B-it-BF16.gguf`):**
  1953	+  - Source: `ggml-org/gemma-4-E2B-it-GGUF` (rev `b4243c156154b6dca9324415f8c7ccc098b4aed1`; publisher offers `BF16` and `Q8_0`, not `F16`)
  1954	+  - Size: `986833664` bytes
  1955	+  - SHA-256: `711e1e8f43fa0664adbac493129be1e6c25b81af4b4cdea97c7d798b25c0a3a4`
  1956	+
  1957	+## Usage
  1958	+
  1959	+Run offline unit tests (no server or network):
  1960	+
  1961	+```bash
  1962	+/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/test_vis0.py -v
  1963	+```
  1964	+
  1965	+Run 2-frame smoke check (requires port 8080 stopped):
  1966	+
  1967	+```bash
  1968	+/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py --limit 2
  1969	+```
  1970	+
  1971	+Run full 10-frame evaluation:
  1972	+
  1973	+```bash
  1974	+/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3 -B benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py
  1975	+```
  1976	+
  1977	+Each run writes `results.json`, `manifest.json`, `server.log`, `run.log`, and `vis0_sheet.pdf` under `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/runs/<UTC>/` and copies `vis0_sheet.pdf` to `/storage/emulated/0/Download/vis0/`.

    22	class FakeServer:
    23	    def __init__(self, budget, label, fail_start=False, alive_sequence=None, log_text=''):
    24	        self.budget = budget
    25	        self.label = label
    26	        self.fail_start = fail_start
    27	        self.alive_sequence = list(alive_sequence or [True])
    28	        self.log_text = log_text
    29	        self.proc = type('FakeProc', (), {'pid': 99999, 'poll': lambda self_: None if self.is_alive() else 139})()
    30	        self.started_utc = f'2026-10-18T00:00:00Z_{label}'
    31	        self.ended_utc = None
    32	        self.stopped = False
    33	
    34	    def start(self):
    35	        if self.fail_start:
    36	            raise RuntimeError('simulated start failure')
    37	
    38	    def stop(self):
    39	        self.stopped = True
    40	        self.ended_utc = f'2026-10-18T00:01:00Z_{self.label}'
    41	
    42	    def is_alive(self):
    43	        if len(self.alive_sequence) > 1:
    44	            return self.alive_sequence.pop(0)
    45	        return self.alive_sequence[0]
    46	
    47	    def sample_vmhwm(self):
    48	        return 2900000
    49	
    50	    def log_size(self):
    51	        return 0
    52	
    53	    def log_delta_from(self, offset):
    54	        return self.log_text[offset:]
    55	
    56	    def log_tail(self, n_lines=25):
    57	        return '\n'.join(self.log_text.splitlines()[-n_lines:])
    58	
    59	    def summary(self):
    60	        return {
    61	            'label': self.label,
    62	            'budget': self.budget,
    63	            'command': vis0.build_server_cmd(budget=self.budget),
    64	            'started_utc': self.started_utc,
    65	            'ended_utc': self.ended_utc,
    66	            'startup_s_not_a_measurement': 1.0,
    67	            'server_pid': 99999,
    68	            'server_name': 'llama-server',
    69	            'peak_vmhwm_kib_not_a_measurement': 2900000,
    70	            'exit_code': 0 if self.is_alive() else 139,
    71	            'timing_label': vis0.TIMING_LABEL,
    72	        }

    99	    def test_encode_image_data_uri_roundtrip(self):
   100	        raw = b'\xff\xd8\xff\xe0fake-jpeg-bytes\xff\xd9'
   101	        uri = vis0.encode_image_data_uri(raw)
   102	        self.assertTrue(uri.startswith('data:image/jpeg;base64,'))

   160	    def test_classify_tokenized_marker_and_verdict(self):
   161	        sample = {
   162	            '<start_of_turn>user': [
   163	                {'id': 236820, 'piece': '<'},
   164	                {'id': 4159, 'piece': 'start'},
   165	                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
   166	                {'id': 1142, 'piece': 'of'},
   167	                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
   168	                {'id': 11438, 'piece': 'turn'},
   169	                {'id': 236813, 'piece': '>'},
   170	                {'id': 4186, 'piece': 'user'},
   171	            ],
   172	            '<end_of_turn>': [
   173	                {'id': 236820, 'piece': '<'},
   174	                {'id': 1711, 'piece': 'end'},
   175	                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
   176	                {'id': 1142, 'piece': 'of'},
   177	                {'id': 236779, 'piece': '_'},
   178	                {'id': 11438, 'piece': 'turn'},
   179	                {'id': 236813, 'piece': '>'},
   180	            ],
   181	            '<|turn>user': [
   182	                {'id': 105, 'piece': '<|turn>'},
   183	                {'id': 2364, 'piece': 'user'},
   184	            ],
   185	            '<turn|>': [
   186	                {'id': 106, 'piece': '<turn|>'},
   187	            ],
   188	            '<|turn>model': [
   189	                {'id': 105, 'piece': '<|turn>'},
   190	                {'id': 4368, 'piece': 'model'},
   191	            ],
   192	        }
   193	        checks = [vis0.classify_tokenized_marker(k, v) for k, v in sample.items()]
   194	        by_in = {c['input']: c for c in checks}
   195	        self.assertFalse(by_in['<start_of_turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
   196	        self.assertFalse(by_in['<end_of_turn>']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
   197	        self.assertTrue(by_in['<|turn>user']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
   198	        self.assertTrue(by_in['<turn|>']['whole_input_is_single_token'])
   199	        self.assertTrue(by_in['<|turn>model']['tag_is_single_special_token'])
   200	
   201	        verdict = vis0.evaluate_template_verdict(
   202	            chat_template="{{ '<|turn>user\\n' + message['content'] + '<turn|>\\n<|turn>model\\n' }}",
   203	            tokenize_results=checks,
   204	            rendered_hi='<|bos|><|turn>user\nHi<turn|>\n<|turn>model\n<|thought\n<|channel|>',
   205	        )
   206	        self.assertEqual(verdict, 'CONFIRMED')

   218	    def test_vision_crash_restarts_at_most_once_and_stops_on_two_crashes(self):
   219	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   220	            frames = []
   221	            for i in range(3):
   222	                p = Path(tmp) / f'f{i}.jpg'
   223	                Image.new('RGB', (32, 32), 'blue').save(p, format='JPEG')
   224	                frames.append({'frame_stem': f'f{i}', 'photo': f'f{i}.jpg', 'frame_path': str(p), 'yolo_lines': ''})
   225	
   226	            created = []
   227	
   228	            def factory(budget, label):
   229	                srv = FakeServer(budget=budget, label=label, alive_sequence=[False], log_text='SIGSEGV\n')
   230	                created.append(srv)
   231	                return srv
   232	
   233	            def crashing_post(path, payload):
   234	                raise ConnectionResetError('connection reset by peer')
   235	
   236	            res = vis0.run_vision_budget_pass(
   237	                budget=280,
   238	                target_frames=frames,
   239	                server_factory=factory,
   240	                post_fn=crashing_post,
   241	                log_fn=lambda _: None,
   242	            )
   243	            self.assertEqual(res['crashes'], 2)
   244	            self.assertTrue(res['stopped_early'])
   245	            self.assertEqual(res['stop_reason'], 'two_consecutive_crashes_at_budget')
   246	            self.assertEqual(len(res['calls']), 2)
   247	            self.assertEqual(len(created), 2)

   328	    def test_http_error_on_live_server_is_not_counted_as_crash(self):
   329	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   330	            frames = []
   331	            for i in range(3):
   332	                p = Path(tmp) / f'f{i}.jpg'
   333	                Image.new('RGB', (32, 32), 'green').save(p, format='JPEG')
   334	                frames.append({'frame_stem': f'f{i}', 'photo': f'f{i}.jpg', 'frame_path': str(p), 'yolo_lines': ''})
   335	
   336	            created = []
   337	
   338	            def factory(budget, label):
   339	                srv = FakeServer(budget=budget, label=label, alive_sequence=[True], log_text='')
   340	                created.append(srv)
   341	                return srv
   342	
   343	            call_idx = {'n': 0}
   344	
   345	            def flaky_http_post(path, payload):
   346	                call_idx['n'] += 1
   347	                if call_idx['n'] <= 2:
   348	                    raise urllib.error.HTTPError(
   349	                        url='http://127.0.0.1:8090/chat/completions',
   350	                        code=400,
   351	                        msg='Bad Request',
   352	                        hdrs=None,
   353	                        fp=None,
   354	                    )
   355	                return {
   356	                    'choices': [{'message': {'content': 'Recovered on frame 2.'}, 'finish_reason': 'stop'}],
   357	                    'usage': {'prompt_tokens': 96, 'completion_tokens': 5},
   358	                    'timings': {'prompt_ms': 100.0, 'predicted_ms': 50.0, 'predicted_per_second': 10.0},
   359	                }
   360	
   361	            res = vis0.run_vision_budget_pass(
   362	                budget=70,
   363	                target_frames=frames,
   364	                server_factory=factory,
   365	                post_fn=flaky_http_post,
   366	                log_fn=lambda _: None,
   367	            )
   368	            self.assertEqual(res['crashes'], 0)
   369	            self.assertFalse(res['stopped_early'])
   370	            self.assertEqual(len(created), 1)
   371	            self.assertEqual([c['status'] for c in res['calls']], ['error', 'error', 'ok'])
   372	            self.assertEqual([c['crashed'] for c in res['calls']], [False, False, False])
   373

   434	    def test_check_port_8080_stopped_rejects_http_503_and_accepts_refused(self):
   435	        with unittest.mock.patch('urllib.request.urlopen') as mock_open, \
   436	                unittest.mock.patch.object(vis0, 'check_port_free') as mock_free:
   437	            mock_open.side_effect = urllib.error.HTTPError(
   438	                url='http://127.0.0.1:8080/health',
   439	                code=503,
   440	                msg='Service Unavailable',
   441	                hdrs=None,
   442	                fp=io.BytesIO(b'{"error":"loading model"}'),
   443	            )
   444	            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as ctx503:
   445	                vis0.check_port_8080_stopped(8080)
   446	            self.assertIn('503', str(ctx503.exception))
   447	            mock_free.assert_not_called()
   448	
   449	            mock_open.side_effect = urllib.error.URLError( TimeoutError('timed out') )
   450	            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as ctxtmo:
   451	                vis0.check_port_8080_stopped(8080)
   452	            self.assertIn('unexpected URLError', str(ctxtmo.exception))
   453	            mock_free.assert_not_called()
   454	
   455	            mock_open.side_effect = urllib.error.URLError(ConnectionRefusedError(111, 'Connection refused'))
   456	            self.assertTrue(vis0.check_port_8080_stopped(8080))
   457	            mock_free.assert_called_once_with(8080)

exec
/bin/bash -lc 'nl -ba docs/DECISIONS.md' in /data/data/com.termux/files/home/review/vis0/round3_copy
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# DECISIONS
     2	
     3	Append-only log of settled tradeoffs. One line per decision plus a short why.
     4	**Never rewrite existing entries** — supersede them with a new numbered entry
     5	that references the old one.
     6	
     7	Format: `NN. [area] decision — why`
     8	
     9	---
    10	
    11	## Model & inference
    12	
    13	1. **Gemma 4 E2B Q4_K_M as the robot default** — 11-12 tok/s vs E4B's 7.2 tok/s
    14	   on the same config; speed matters more than depth for navigation. (`server_manager.py: setup_q4`)
    15	2. **Gemma 4 E4B kept as an opt-in quality mode, not the default** — measured 9.9s
    16	   warm cycle vs E2B's 5.1s; too slow for the control loop, good for chat. (`setup_e4b`)
    17	3. **Q4_K_M over Q3_K_S for E4B** — Q3_K_S measured *slower* (5.8 vs 7.2 tok/s)
    18	   despite being smaller; llama.cpp's ARM NEON kernels are optimised for Q4_K_M.
    19	4. **4 threads, not 8** — token generation is memory-bandwidth bound on Tensor G2;
    20	   8 threads pulled in the slow LITTLE cores and dropped throughput.
    21	5. **`--parallel 1`** — default 4 parallel slots split the KV cache and cut
    22	   single-request speed.
    23	6. **`--swa-full` instead of `--cache-ram 0`** — Gemma 4's sliding-window attention
    24	   was invalidating the prompt cache every request, forcing a full ~15s reprocess
    25	   of the system prompt. `--swa-full` restores cache reuse; repeat-prefix prompt
    26	   eval dropped from ~15s to ~0.1s. (Supersedes the earlier `--cache-ram 0`
    27	   workaround; the comment in `server_manager.py` is stale and still mentions it.)
    28	7. **`--mlock` rejected** — pinned the model in RAM but measured no speed gain and
    29	   left only ~233 MB free, which is unsafe alongside YOLO and the camera.
    30	8. **mmproj file deleted** — Gemma 4 has native vision; the separate multimodal
    31	   projector was redundant, freed ~940 MB.
    32	9. **Both LoRA adapters rejected (voice and nav)** — fine-tuned adapters scored
    33	   *worse* than the base model (voice 80% vs 83%, nav 31% vs 38%); base Gemma 4 was
    34	   already at the task ceiling and the adapters degraded generalisation.
    35	10. **OpenCL GPU backend abandoned** — `libOpenCL.so` exists on the device but
    36	    returns "platform IDs not available" from Termux userspace; the OpenCL-enabled
    37	    build was also slower than the CPU-only build, so it was rebuilt without it.
    38	11. **KleidiAI / SVE / SME2 path not viable** — Tensor G2 lacks those ARM
    39	    extensions (`/proc/cpuinfo` shows asimd/asimddp only).
    40	
    41	## Vision
    42	
    43	12. **yolo11m.onnx as the detection model** — chosen over lighter variants for
    44	    accuracy on indoor scenes; runs per-cycle at 640×640. (`detect_person.py`)
    45	13. **CONF 0.35 / IOU 0.45** — detection and NMS thresholds as coded.
    46	14. **Coarse distance from bounding-box area, not a depth model** — area ratio
    47	    buckets (>0.3 very close … >0.03 medium) are free and good enough for the
    48	    Python safety rules; stereo/known-height estimation handles the finer cases.
    49	15. **Position from horizontal thirds of the 640px frame** — cx<213 left,
    50	    cx>427 right, else center. Simple and deterministic.
    51	16. **Single-photo distance via known real-world object heights** — avoids needing
    52	    a second camera; `REAL_HEIGHTS` table in `stereo_depth.py`.
    53	17. **Stereo disparity below 2px is discarded** — below that the triangulation is
    54	    noise; falls back to the single-photo estimate.
    55	18. **FOCAL_PX = 500 is a placeholder, not a measurement** — flagged
    56	    "calibrate later" in the source; all derived distances are approximate until
    57	    a known-distance calibration is done.
    58	
    59	## Navigation architecture
    60	
    61	19. **Dual-rate split: Python for deterministic rules, Gemma for strategy only** —
    62	    benchmarking Gemma on rules it should never see scored 55%; moving person /
    63	    obstacle / room-arrival decisions into Python and leaving Gemma only the
    64	    exploration and mission-complete calls scored 93%.
    65	20. **Gemma is called on triggers, not every cycle** — interval, person seen, goal
    66	    reached, or new room mapped; keeps both latency and heat down.
    67	21. **Vision (image payload) sent on a narrower trigger set than text** — images
    68	    cost far more prompt tokens, so only the every-5 / person / goal / new-room
    69	    cases attach a photo.
    70	22. **Room identity inferred from single signature objects** — refrigerator→kitchen,
    71	    couch|tv→living room, bed→bedroom, toilet→bathroom. Cheap and sufficient; a
    72	    trained room classifier was deferred.
    73	23. **`nav_sim.py` left as a standalone simulation on Qwen 2.5 3B** — it predates
    74	    the Gemma pipeline and is kept for thermal/loop experiments, not merged into
    75	    the live path.
    76	
    77	## Voice
    78	
    79	24. **Whisper.cpp `ggml-base.bin` retained over Gemma 4's native audio input** —
    80	    Gemma audio landed in llama.cpp but needs the deleted mmproj re-downloaded
    81	    (~940 MB) and had reported stability crashes; the working Whisper path was not
    82	    disturbed.
    83	25. **Fixed 5-second capture window** — simple and predictable; VAD-based silence
    84	    trimming deferred.
    85	26. **JSON action array as the command contract** — `PARSE_SYS` constrains Gemma to
    86	    emit only a JSON array, with `[` pre-injected into the prompt to force the
    87	    format.
    88	
    89	## Platform & safety
    90	
    91	27. **Root restored via Magisk `Select and patch a file`, not the old images** —
    92	    an OTA to Android 17 (CP2A.260705.006) overwrote the Magisk boot patch. The
    93	    March boot images in Downloads were from an older build; anti-rollback on
    94	    recent bootloaders makes flashing an older Android 16 image a brick risk, so
    95	    they were deleted and a fresh init_boot from the matching A17 factory image
    96	    was patched and flashed.
    97	28. **Only `init_boot` is ever flashed** — smallest recoverable operation; the
    98	    stock A17 `init_boot.img` is kept on the laptop as the rescue file.
    99	29. **BIG-core thermal zone (zone9) is the thermal signal** — battery temperature
   100	    lags SoC heat by a minute or more and dampens the swing; token-rate collapse
   101	    and zone9 both track the real throttle.
   102	30. **Rest between inference calls makes the system *faster*, not slower** —
   103	    measured 0s rest → 8.7 tok/s sustained with throttling, 2s rest → 11.1 tok/s
   104	    with only 6% droop, because the chip stops self-throttling.
   105	
   106	## Hardware
   107	
   108	31. **2× MX1508 chosen over the L298N** — two dual H-bridges cover all four motors,
   109	    no screw terminals, and no separate enable pins needed.
   110	32. **MX1508 speed control is PWM on the IN pins** — the part has no ENA/ENB;
   111	    forward = PWM on IN1 with IN2 low, reverse = the inverse.
   112	33. **GPIO map: 16/17/18/19 → front pair, 21/22/23/25 → rear pair.**
   113	34. **2S 18650 pack behind an HW-391 20A BMS** — pack measured 8.2 V with cells
   114	    balanced at 4.1 V / 4.1 V.
   115	35. **Separate buck converters for logic and sensors** — 5 V rail for the ESP32
   116	    (set to 5.03 V no-load before connecting anything), 3.3 V rail for sensors.
   117	36. **Star ground required at final assembly** — a marginal ground return on the
   118	    buck path produced a false 3.76 V reading on the ESP32 3V3 pin and left GPIO2
   119	    floating high; both vanish on USB power, confirming the board itself is fine.
   120	37. **EQV 4WD mecanum kit, 8.2V pack is the intended chassis voltage** — motors
   121	    rated 1.5–12V (nominal 6.0V/100rpm, kit designed for 7–12V external), so the
   122	    8.2V rail is safe by design. Caveat: MX1508 tops out ~10V, so 8.2V runs the
   123	    driver near its ceiling (~82%), and nominal-6V motors at 8.2V sustained run
   124	    hot — may cap effective duty to ~6V-equivalent for longevity once driving.
   125	    (Bench MOTOR_SPEED 130 ≈ 51% duty is gentle.)
   126	38. **Decoupling caps are mandatory, not optional** — 100µF electrolytic + 0.1µF
   127	    ceramic across each MX1508 VCC/GND, plus 470–1000µF bulk at the P+ junction.
   128	    Without them, PWM motor-current spikes collapse the 8.2V rail; the buck feeding
   129	    the ESP32 from that rail brownout-resets the MCU every PWM cycle, producing a
   130	    start-stall-retry stutter. Isolated by elimination: motor direct to pack, to
   131	    BMS, and via MX1508 with a hardwired IN1 jumper all ran continuously; ESP32 DC
   132	    drive ran continuously; ESP32 PWM failed on buck power but ran clean on USB.
   133	    Root cause was shared-rail brownout — not wire gauge, not the drivers, not the
   134	    code.
   135	39. **Interim bench/drive config until caps arrive** — ESP32 powered from phone USB
   136	    (supply independent of the motor rail), motors from the battery rail via the
   137	    MX1508s. Buck→VIN stays disconnected. Verified: all four motors run continuously
   138	    under PWM (1kHz, 8-bit, speed 130) in this configuration.
   139	40. **Physical driver assignment is LEFT/RIGHT, not front/rear** — supersedes the
   140	    wording of #33; the GPIO map itself is unchanged. MX1508 #1 = right side:
   141	    chA P16/P17 = rear right, chB P18/P19 = front right. MX1508 #2 = left side:
   142	    chA P21/P22 = rear left, chB P23/P25 = front left. Motor index for mecanum
   143	    mixing: FL=3, FR=1, RL=2, RR=0.
   144	
   145	## Navigation (cont.)
   146	
   147	41. **The 45cm obstacle threshold has no benchmark source — do not apply** — it is
   148	    inferred from `nav_logic_test.py:162` test "obstacle center" (40cm, expects
   149	    BACK), which fails under the function's default `obstacle_dist=25`; 45 is
   150	    back-derived to make that one case pass, not measured. Moot regardless — no
   151	    distance sensor is fitted (see STATUS "Pending").
   152	
   153	## Hardware (cont.)
   154	
   155	42. **HC-SR04 ultrasonic fitted and verified** — TRIG on P27 direct (3.3V drive is
   156	    sufficient for the trigger); ECHO on P26 via a 1kΩ/2kΩ divider (5V × 2/3 =
   157	    3.33V) — the divider is mandatory, ECHO is a 5V output into a 3.3V pin. Sensor
   158	    VCC from the ESP32 5V/VIN pin (phone USB), not the 3.3V rail and not buck #2,
   159	    so the sensor stays off the motor rail. Readings verified against a tape
   160	    measure. Supersedes the "moot regardless" caveat in #41 — the threshold
   161	    question is live again once `HAS_ULTRASONIC` is enabled, and 45 is still
   162	    unsourced.
   163	43. **Driver #2 channel-to-corner assignment corrected** — supersedes #40's
   164	    left-side detail; the GPIO map and the left/right split are unchanged.
   165	    Measured by driving each channel alone: ch0 P16/P17 = RR, ch1 P18/P19 = FR,
   166	    ch2 P21/P22 = FRONT left, ch3 P23/P25 = REAR left. #40 had the left pair
   167	    reversed. Left motors spin backward on positive drive, so indices 2 and 3 are
   168	    inverted in firmware. Motor index map: RR=0, FR=1, FL=2, RL=3.
   169	    (Verified in `mode1_simple.ino`.)
   170	
   171	## Serial transport
   172	
   173	44. **Board is CP2102 (`10c4:ea60`), not CH340** — endpoints 0x01 OUT / 0x81 IN;
   174	    init is `IFC_ENABLE`(0x00) → `SET_BAUDRATE`(0x1E, 32-bit value) →
   175	    `LINE_CTL` 8N1(0x03). `usb.util.claim_interface()` is mandatory — without it
   176	    control transfers succeed and every bulk read times out silently. DTR/RTS left
   177	    untouched; asserting them resets the ESP32. `motors.py` transport rewritten;
   178	    CH340 version backed up at `motors.py.ch340.bak`. The old transport could never
   179	    have worked with this board, so no serial command from the phone had ever
   180	    round-tripped before this session.
   181	45. **Baud 115200, chosen not inherited** — CP210x sets the rate as an explicit
   182	    32-bit value, so the CH340 divisor-table reasoning that produced 9600 no longer
   183	    applies. Verified phone-side and in the Arduino serial monitor.
   184	46. **All USB motor operations require root plus an explicit `LD_LIBRARY_PATH`** —
   185	    `/dev/bus/usb` is not readable by Termux's app UID, and root does not inherit
   186	    Termux's environment. Applies to `motors.py`, `teleop.py`, `main.py`,
   187	    `run_mission.py` — not to the LLM server, benchmarks, or vision scripts.
   188	
   189	## Firmware
   190	
   191	47. **`WATCHDOG_MS` = 1000, not 5000** — a dropped link must not leave the chassis
   192	    driving for five seconds. Paired with a host-side keepalive: `motors.py`'s
   193	    duration helpers re-send the command every 200 ms via `_hold()`, so moves longer
   194	    than the watchdog are not cut short. Verified — a 3.0 s forward runs 3.1 s,
   195	    continuous.
   196	48. **Diagonal commands `FWD_L` / `FWD_R` / `BACK_L` / `BACK_R` added** — the
   197	    mecanum mixing already handled them; only the command names were missing. Each
   198	    drives one diagonal pair — two wheels, not four, is correct.
   199	49. **`mode2_auto.ino` corner map corrected to match #43** — RR=0, FR=1, FL=2, RL=3,
   200	    with `INVERT` indices 2 and 3 true. It had been carrying the superseded #40
   201	    mapping with `INVERT` all false, so some commands drove the wrong physical wheel
   202	    and one left motor ran backwards. Plausible cause of the `BACK_R` single-wheel
   203	    fault — NOT yet re-tested after the fix.
   204	50. **`DIST:` emits -1 when no echo returns within ~4 m** — so the host can
   205	    distinguish "nothing in range" from "very close". The previous build emitted
   206	    nothing in that case, leaving the last reading stale.
   207	
   208	## Navigation (cont. II)
   209	
   210	51. **`Robot.get_distance()` sanitises the sensor** — -1 maps to 400 (clear to
   211	    sensor max), and a reading older than 1.0 s maps to 999 (unusable, treated as
   212	    clear). Without this, `-1 < 15` is true and an open corridor triggers BACK on
   213	    every cycle — a bug that was latent only while distance was pinned at 999.
   214	52. **`navigate_rules` rewritten against live sensor input** — a blocked path
   215	    strafes first (mecanum holds heading, so YOLO keeps the same view), rotates on
   216	    axis if strafing isn't clearing, and sets `nav_stuck` after repeated avoidance
   217	    so `run_cycle` hands the strategy call to Gemma. This is a rewrite, not a port
   218	    of `nav_logic_test.py` — see #55.
   219	53. **Gemma may not override a Python safety move** — `run_cycle` previously
   220	    overwrote `navigate_rules`' output unconditionally, so an obstacle BACK could be
   221	    replaced by the model's FORWARD, a direct violation of #19 that was harmless
   222	    only while distance was pinned at 999. Safety moves now bypass the Gemma call
   223	    entirely unless `nav_stuck` is set.
   224	54. **`OBSTACLE_DIST` stays 25 cm** — coast distance measured as effectively zero
   225	    (gearmotors, duty 130), so the binding constraint is sensing latency (200 ms
   226	    ping interval plus cycle time), not braking. 25 cm carries adequate margin.
   227	    #41 stands: 45 remains unsourced and rejected.
   228	
   229	## Benchmarks
   230	
   231	55. **`nav_logic_test.py`'s 93% does not describe `main.py`** — it matched the
   232	    literal string `'obstacle'` in synthetic scene text, a label YOLO cannot emit,
   233	    and ran with no distance sensor. The live rules take obstacles from
   234	    `get_distance()`. `main.py`'s nav logic is currently UNBENCHMARKED.
   235	
   236	## Platform (cont.)
   237	
   238	56. **Four bare `except:` clauses in `main.py` were swallowing KeyboardInterrupt** —
   239	    Ctrl+C during a Gemma call returned a default move and the loop continued. Fixed
   240	    to re-raise. This was the emergency stop.
   241	57. **`llama-server` and `teleop.py` both bind port 8080 and cannot run
   242	    concurrently** — autonomous is unaffected; `main.py` is a client of the server,
   243	    it binds nothing.
   244	
   245	## Scope
   246	
   247	58. **Mode 2 (video/audio teleop) parked indefinitely** — MJPEG encoding via IP
   248	    Webcam alongside the AP and USB-OTG heats the Pixel enough to compete with
   249	    llama.cpp and YOLO for thermal headroom. Not a primary objective. Firmware and
   250	    control path are mode-agnostic, so nothing is lost by parking it. Note: this
   251	    parks the video/audio path only. `mode2_auto.ino` is the serial-command firmware
   252	    for both teleop and autonomous and is unaffected by the name.
   253	
   254	## Navigation (cont. III)
   255	
   256	59. **Avoidance escalation uses an explicit counter (`blocked_n`), not state
   257	    inferred from `last_moves`** — supersedes #52's `nav_stuck`-on-repeated-
   258	    avoidance mechanism. The first implementation read `last_moves[-4:]` and
   259	    flipped `avoid_side` whenever four avoidance moves were present — true on every
   260	    subsequent cycle, so it alternated LEFT/RIGHT forever (rotations cancelling each
   261	    other) and set `nav_stuck` every cycle, calling Gemma at ~5s per cycle. Found by
   262	    `nav_test.py`, never ran on hardware.
   263	60. **The robot never REVERSES to avoid an obstacle** — with one forward-facing
   264	    sensor, BACK drives fully blind and away from the only direction the robot can
   265	    see. Rotation on axis translates the chassis not at all and is always safe while
   266	    blocked. Strafing is blind sideways and is NOT covered by this rule — see #61.
   267	    Applies to both the `<15` branch and the ladder terminal. Revisit if the six IR
   268	    sensors (side + rear) are fitted.
   269	61. **Escalation ladder: 2 strafes, 4 rotations one way, flip side, 6 rotations back
   270	    through and past centre, then restart** — blocked continuously for 60s
   271	    (`ESCAPE_TIMEOUT`) → STOP. Time-based, not cycle-based, because cycle duration
   272	    varies widely once Gemma calls land. The two opening strafes are a DELIBERATE
   273	    bounded risk, not an oversight: mecanum holds heading so YOLO keeps the same
   274	    view (#52), which rotation destroys. They are blind sideways — capped at 2
   275	    cycles for that reason, and the first thing the side IR sensors should make
   276	    sensed. Do not remove them citing #60; #60 is about BACK. Supersedes #52's
   277	    strafe-then-rotate sequencing, which had no step counts.
   278	62. **Gemma is consulted once per blockage episode, at the side-flip, guarded by
   279	    `asked_gemma`** — supersedes #52's per-avoidance escalation: Gemma is consulted
   280	    once per blockage episode, not on every avoidance. The flag clears only when the
   281	    path actually clears. Chosen over per-cycle consultation (thermal cost, defeats
   282	    #20) and over never re-asking. Interim — revisit once the loop runs on hardware.
   283	
   284	## Testing
   285	
   286	63. **`nav_test.py` calls `navigate_rules` directly with synthetic distances** — the
   287	    obstacle branches cannot be reached in `run_mission.py --dry` because
   288	    `self.motors` is None pins distance at 999, so every safety path was unexercised
   289	    until this harness existed. It found #59.
   290	
   291	## Calibration
   292	
   293	64. **Forward motion is modelled as IT1 with dead time** — velocity responds to a
   294	    PWM step as PT1 (drivetrain inertia), position is its integral.
   295	    `G(s) = K_I/(s(T1·s+1))·e^(−Tt·s)`. Feedforward only — there is no odometry, so
   296	    the loop cannot be closed during normal operation. On synthetic data (K_I
   297	    42cm/s, T1 0.18s, Tt 0.09s), commanding a 10cm move from the naive d/v duration
   298	    travels 1.98cm — an 80% distance shortfall, equivalently a 52% duration error.
   299	    At 100cm the duration error falls to 10%. So the transient dominates exactly at
   300	    the short corrective moves the ladder issues most. Source: `identify_it1.py`'s
   301	    duration table. `identify_it1.py` (numpy-only grid fit, K_I solved in closed
   302	    form) validated on synthetic data: recovered 42.06/0.1788/0.0953 against true
   303	    42/0.18/0.09.
   304	65. **`DIST_EVERY_MS` 200 is adequate for K_I but not for T1/Tt** — the whole
   305	    transient is 2-3 samples and Tt's quantisation error exceeds Tt. Use 50ms (and
   306	    `ECHO_TIMEOUT` 12000µs, ~2m) for calibration runs. `log_run.py` dedupes on
   307	    `dist_at` so it records true sensor sample times, and warns when the interval is
   308	    too coarse to fit the transient. CALIBRATION BUILDS ONLY. Restore `ECHO_TIMEOUT`
   309	    to 25000 before driving: at 12000µs the sensor's max range is ~2m, so `DIST:-1`
   310	    means ">=2m" while #50 defines it as ">=4m" and #51 maps it to 400cm — the
   311	    sanitiser would assert 4m of clearance on 2m of evidence. Not hazardous at the
   312	    25cm threshold, since both readings are "clear", but it feeds a false number to
   313	    `GEMMA_SYS` and would become hazardous if any rule ever compares against a long
   314	    distance.
   315	
   316	## Licensing & docs
   317	
   318	66. **`LICENSES.md` is canonical for the licence allowlist; `APP_CLAUDE.md` §0.5
   319	    carries a derived copy on purpose** — Claude Code auto-reads only `CLAUDE.md`,
   320	    so a pointer to a second file would sometimes go unread, and an unread
   321	    allowlist is how an AGPL dependency gets added. Duplication accepted; drift
   322	    handled by marking the copy derived: on mismatch `LICENSES.md` wins and the
   323	    agent reports rather than reconciles.
   324	
   325	## App blockers
   326	
   327	67. **Blocker A1 passes: root-free USB Host serial confirmed** — Android 17, unrooted
   328	    app sandbox. Verified with *Serial USB Terminal* (kai-morich) against an ESP32-S3
   329	    via CH343 bridge (`1a86:55d3`), 115200 8N1: `PING` → `ALIVE` in 5 ms, with the
   330	    library asserting DTR and resetting the board at connect (boot banner, then
   331	    `READY` 275 ms later) — so control transfers work, not just stream reads. Tested
   332	    on the S3 rather than the robot's CP2102 because the blocker is the Android
   333	    sandbox, not the bridge chip, and the CP2102 path is already proven on this phone
   334	    with root (#44). Residual CP2102 work is a manifest VID:PID filter plus driver
   335	    selection — a follow-up, not a gate.
   336	68. **Blocker A2 passes: Gemma sustained throughput inside a real app process** —
   337	    Gemma 4 E2B Q4_K_M sustains 7.4-7.6 tok/s for 10 minutes in an unrooted app
   338	    process (PocketPal AI, screen on), stable with no measurable degradation. Below
   339	    the Termux baseline of 11-12 but stable, and the shortfall is attributed to the
   340	    absence of a `--swa-full` equivalent: PocketPal exposes no such flag, so Gemma's
   341	    sliding-window attention invalidates the prompt cache every turn (#6). The
   342	    shipped app drives llama.cpp directly and regains the flag, so 7.6 is treated as
   343	    a floor for the app, not a ceiling — predicted, not measured. Runner defaults
   344	    were actively hostile — 6 CPU threads (violates #4) and a 131072 context — and
   345	    cost roughly 40% of throughput before correction; the app must set threads,
   346	    context and cache flags in code, never inherit defaults. zone9 peaked at 82 °C,
   347	    median 66 °C; that reading is routed to the Local AI thread as corroborating
   348	    evidence only and does not fill STATUS.md's outstanding loaded measurement.
   349	69. **The app requires a foreground service, not merely a wake lock — a liveness
   350	    requirement, not an optimisation** — observed during A2: PocketPal's generation
   351	    stopped the moment the app lost focus, while Termux's concurrent logger survived,
   352	    the difference being the foreground service Termux holds. Without one, the
   353	    reasoning layer dies on any notification, call or app switch mid-mission. Not a
   354	    safety defect: rule 0.2 keeps drive, obstacle stop and emergency stop below the
   355	    reasoning layer, and the 1000 ms firmware watchdog (#47) sits below the app
   356	    entirely. Likely manifest type is `connectedDevice`, since the app is genuinely
   357	    driving attached hardware. Screen-off throughput under a partial wake lock is NOT
   358	    measured — A2's 7.4-7.6 was screen-on, and equal performance must not be assumed.
   359	
   360	## Inference (cont.)
   361	
   362	70. **#10's OpenCL rejection is scoped to Termux userspace, not Android GPU access
   363	    generally** — the app runtime decision reopens GPU/NPU via vendor-supported
   364	    paths. #10 stands for the prototype; it does not bind the app. Scoping only: no
   365	    path is chosen and nothing is measured, so this authorises the investigation
   366	    rather than the use.
   367	
   368	## Model & inference (cont.)
   369	
   370	71. **Gemma 4 QAT confirmed real and official, but the "~1 GB E2B" footprint claim
   371	    is unsupported for a llama.cpp deployment** — verified against Google's model
   372	    card and ai.google.dev/gemma/docs/core: official Gemma 4 QAT collection exists
   373	    (Apache-2.0, E2B included), QAT holds near-bf16 quality at 4-bit. BUT: the QAT
   374	    Q4_0 GGUF is the same 4-bit width as the deployed q4_k_m, so it lands near the
   375	    current 3.3 GB, not 1 GB (E4B QAT Q4_0 GGUF measures 5.15 GB on HF for
   376	    reference). The ~1 GB figure can only refer to the "mobile-optimized" wNa8o8
   377	    format (2-bit decode layers), and NO evidence was found that llama.cpp can load
   378	    wNa8o8 — it is described as a custom schema for vLLM-class runtimes, not GGUF.
   379	    Conclusion: the QAT-for-footprint premise is blocked on inference-engine format
   380	    support and must be confirmed before any download. QAT-at-equal-size
   381	    (quality/robustness at ~3.3 GB) remains a legitimate thing to benchmark. Do not
   382	    swap the default on footprint grounds.
   383	72. **MTP speculative decoding requires a QAT drafter at the same precision as the
   384	    target** — per Google's QAT model card, the drafter must itself be a QAT
   385	    checkpoint matching the target's precision. MTP cannot pair with the current
   386	    q4_k_m model — the whole chain must be QAT. This supersedes the reasoning (not
   387	    the conclusion) behind #4/#10's bandwidth argument only insofar as MTP shares
   388	    the target KV cache rather than running a separate draft model; whether it
   389	    helps a bandwidth-bound phone is still unmeasured. Strictly downstream of a QAT
   390	    model being on the device.
   391	73. **The documented llama.cpp rollback path in COMMANDS.md §9 is broken** —
   392	    `llama-server.swafix` is a 5.9 KB fragment, not a runnable binary. Root cause:
   393	    the build is shared-library, so the on-disk `llama-server` is a ~5.9 KB launcher
   394	    that links against `libllama-server-impl.so` (7.9 MB), `libllama.so.0.0.1609`
   395	    (3.4 MB), `libllama-common.so.0.0.1609` (6.2 MB); copying only the launcher
   396	    captures nothing. `llama-server.working` (12 MB) is a valid earlier
   397	    statically-linked binary and is a genuine standalone fallback, but it PREDATES
   398	    the `--swa-full` fix (#6) — rolling back to it silently loses SWA. Correct
   399	    rollback for a shared-lib build is the whole `build/` tree. Established this
   400	    session: `build-b1609.tar.gz` (49 MB, verified: all four critical files
   401	    present, gzip integrity OK) archives commit e1a1abb7 / version 1609. Recovery:
   402	    `cd ~/llama.cpp && rm -rf build && tar xzf ~/build-b1609.tar.gz`. Second path:
   403	    rebuild from git commit e1a1abb7.
   404	
   405	## Thermal (cont.)
   406	
   407	74. **Loaded BIG-core temperature measured for the first time: 97-101 °C sustained
   408	    under llama.cpp inference, against 31-38 °C idle** — zone9 confirmed as `BIG`
   409	    by reading `/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone9/type`, so #29 stands. The
   410	    kernel's own trip points for that zone, as read: active 20/55/80 °C, PASSIVE
   411	    100 °C, active 104/106/110 °C, hot 120 °C. The device therefore runs in
   412	    thermal equilibrium exactly at its designed passive throttle point — 11.5
   413	    tok/s is what the envelope permits, not what the silicon could do cold. 20 °C
   414	    of margin remains above that. Die temperature is not skin temperature: during
   415	    the same run `disp_therm` read 29.4 °C and `usb_pwr_therm2` 29.6 °C, which is
   416	    why the phone feels cool at 100 °C junction. Silicon has almost no thermal
   417	    mass, so it swings ~60 °C within a single inference burst and falls back
   418	    during rest — idle and loaded readings are both correct and not in conflict.
   419	    Supersedes STATUS's unsourced 79-82 °C figure.
   420	75. **The "two thermal thresholds in circulation" problem is worse than
   421	    recorded: `thermal_guard.py` (warn 82 / critical 86) and `get_temp.py` are
   422	    BOTH orphans** — nothing imports either, and `main.py` uses its own inline
   423	    `get_temp`. So 82/86 are not competing live values, they are dead code. Only
   424	    `run_mission`'s inline >80 °C pause runs. Against a measured 97-101 °C
   425	    operating band that pause fires on essentially every check, so it is not
   426	    "likely too low" (STATUS's wording) but far below the normal range. The
   427	    threshold lives in exactly one place, which makes the fix simple; the correct
   428	    value is NOT yet determined and must come from a real mission run, not this
   429	    bench measurement.
   430	
   431	## Inference (cont. II)
   432	
   433	76. **#30's rest-improves-throughput finding reproduces in direction but NOT in
   434	    magnitude on build 1609** — measured, same harness, 15 cycles: 2 s rest →
   435	    11.5 tok/s median; 0 s rest → 11.1 tok/s median, a 3.5% gap. #30 recorded 8.7
   436	    vs 11.1, a 28% gap. The mechanism is real — within the no-rest run, cycles
   437	    1-5 averaged 11.4 and cycles 11-15 averaged 10.8, while the rested run stayed
   438	    flat — but the effect size is an order of magnitude smaller than recorded.
   439	    Cause not established; candidates are a different llama.cpp build,
   440	    ambient/charging state (the device was on mains), or a longer original run
   441	    reaching a steady state our 15 cycles did not. Treat "2 s rest" as a
   442	    reasonable default, not a tuned constant.
   443	77. **llama.cpp current upstream builds clean on-device and #6 still holds; NOT
   444	    deployed** — built commit 4d917609 (version 0.4.0-dev, build 2233), 624
   445	    commits ahead of e1a1abb7, from clean configure in 6m50s with
   446	    `-DGGML_OPENCL=OFF` per #10. `--swa-full` is still accepted and still reuses
   447	    the prompt cache: an identical repeat request processed 1 prompt token
   448	    instead of 45, on both builds. Cold prompt eval improved (2435 ms → 1035 ms
   449	    for 45 tokens). Generation tok/s was NOT compared honestly — the two
   450	    single-sample readings (14.3 vs 12.2) were taken at different thermal states
   451	    and are not a benchmark. Version reporting changed format:
   452	    `0.4.0-dev (build 2233, commit …)` where b1609 reported only
   453	    `version: 1609`. The robot default stays b1609: the upgrade is proven safe to
   454	    build and proven to preserve #6, not proven better under the real workload
   455	    (long prompts, vision payloads, the nav loop), and nothing currently requires
   456	    it.
   457	
   458	## Vision (cont.)
   459	
   460	78. **`FOCAL_PX` cannot be calibrated from the `bench_photos` set, and at
   461	    `FOCAL_PX`=500 the `PERSON_STOP_DIST` branch is unreachable** — measured
   462	    across 12 person photos: `estimate_distance_single` overshoots by +55% to
   463	    +1000%, every error in the same direction, minimum estimate 164 cm against
   464	    an 80 cm threshold — so the person-stop branch cannot fire. A person at an
   465	    estimated 45 cm reads as 497 cm. In practice people are handled by the 25 cm
   466	    ultrasonic obstacle branch as generic obstacles, so the robot does not hit
   467	    them, but person-aware behaviour does not exist. Calibration was not
   468	    possible from this data: distances were visual estimates (±30-50 cm, stated
   469	    in `labels.csv`, NOT tape-measured as intended), the subject was reclining
   470	    so bbox height does not track the 170 cm standing assumption in
   471	    `REAL_HEIGHTS`, and focal correlates with distance (r = +0.74), indicating
   472	    the pinhole model is violated rather than the data being noisy. Derived
   473	    median 192 fits no better (−40% to +324%). `FOCAL_PX` stays 500 and stays
   474	    UNCALIBRATED. Do not substitute 192.
   475	79. **Person-stop moves from computed centimetres to the coarse area bucket** —
   476	    `PERSON_STOP_DIST` in cm depends on `FOCAL_PX`, which #78 shows cannot be
   477	    calibrated, and on a standing-and-fully-visible pose the robot will rarely
   478	    see indoors. The area-ratio bucket (field `r[3]`, DECISIONS #14) is already
   479	    computed every cycle, needs no calibration, and degrades sensibly on partial
   480	    or seated subjects. Chosen over recalibrating (fixes only the standing case)
   481	    and over widening the ultrasonic rule (loses person-awareness entirely). NOT
   482	    YET IMPLEMENTED — `navigate_rules` still calls `estimate_distance_single`.
   483	    The bucket threshold ('very close' vs 'close') is not yet chosen and must be
   484	    picked against real photos.
   485	80. **`detect_scene()` resizes to 640×640 WITHOUT preserving aspect ratio** — a
   486	    4080×3072 source scales 0.157 horizontally and 0.208 vertically, so bbox
   487	    height is inflated relative to a true-aspect resize, and portrait sources
   488	    distort the other way — the same subject at the same distance yields a ~33%
   489	    different pixel height depending on phone orientation. Since
   490	    `navigate_rules` feeds that height (`r[7]`) to `estimate_distance_single`,
   491	    this is a second and independent reason the cm-based person distance is
   492	    unreliable, compounding #18 and #78. Also recorded: the `detect_scene`
   493	    docstring (`detect_person.py:50`) documents a 4-field tuple; the function
   494	    returns 8 fields.
   495	
   496	## Navigation (cont. IV)
   497	
   498	81. **Gemma can still override a safety move at the one moment it matters
   499	    most — OPEN DEFECT, not fixed** — `run_cycle`'s guard reads
   500	    `use_gemma = (… or stuck) and not (safety_move and not stuck)`. At ladder
   501	    step n==7 `nav_stuck` is set, so `safety_move` and `stuck` are both true and
   502	    the guard admits the Gemma call; the parse loop then overwrites `move` with
   503	    the model's output, including FORWARD, with no re-check afterwards. The
   504	    exposed window is exactly: distance under `OBSTACLE_DIST`, robot boxed in,
   505	    both sweep directions exhausted. This is the case #53 was written to
   506	    prevent; the step-73 patch closed the wide hole and left a narrow one at
   507	    maximum danger. Found by static review, NOT by `nav_test.py`, which
   508	    exercises `navigate_rules` in isolation and never reaches `run_cycle`. Fix
   509	    must re-check the move after the Gemma block, and must be verified against
   510	    `nav_test.py` plus a `run_cycle`-level test that does not yet exist.
   511	
   512	## Tooling
   513	
   514	82. **Claude Code runs on-device via proot-distro Debian, not AVF** — AVF was
   515	    rejected on two grounds independent of availability: it emulates no USB host
   516	    controller, and it shares only `/mnt/shared`, so it can reach neither the
   517	    ESP32 nor `~/robot` — the two things wanted.
   518	    (`getprop ro.virtualization.supported` also returned empty on this device.)
   519	    proot-distro Debian + the official installer gives Claude Code 2.1.261
   520	    inside Termux's own namespace; `proot-distro login debian --bind
   521	    /data/data/com.termux/files/home:/termux-home` exposes the real working
   522	    tree. Confirmed limits: no `su`, no `/dev/bus/usb`, so `motors.py`,
   523	    `teleop.py`, `run_mission.py`, `log_run.py`, `dist_raw.py` and
   524	    `cp2102_test.py` remain manual. Confirmed value: it reads whole files at
   525	    once, which is the reviewing thread's blind spot — it produced the
   526	    dependency map in `FILES.md` and found #81 in a single pass. Its limit is
   527	    that it reasons from the files it is given: it reported
   528	    `Robot.run_mission()` as dead because nothing in `main.py` calls it, while
   529	    `run_mission.py` does. Scope cross-file questions explicitly.
   530	
   531	## Navigation (cont. V)
   532	
   533	83. **Mecanum mixing uses a flipped vx sign relative to the textbook mix —
   534	    verification predates the current wiring** — both `mode1_simple.ino` and
   535	    `mode2_auto.ino` compute `fl = vy-vx+w`, `fr = vy+vx-w`, `rl = vy+vx+w`,
   536	    `rr = vy-vx-w`, with vx's sign reversed from the standard formula, because
   537	    this chassis's rollers sit in a mirrored X pattern rather than the textbook
   538	    arrangement. Verified live on `mode1_remote.ino` (recovered artifact,
   539	    7956 bytes, last modified after a camera-panel edit that postdates the
   540	    verification but did not touch `drive()`): forward and rotate were already
   541	    correct, strafe came out reversed, and only the vx signs were changed.
   542	    `mode1_remote.ino` was forked into `mode1_simple.ino`; its own corner map
   543	    (`FL=3, FR=1, RL=2, RR=0`, `INVERT` all-false) is the pre-#43 mapping and
   544	    is NOT safe to flash as a reference build — it is a provenance record only.
   545	    `mode2_auto.ino` carries the same equation by copy, not by independent
   546	    verification, and has never been run with motors. Re-verification of
   547	    strafe and diagonals under the #43 mapping is OUTSTANDING. Not a suspected
   548	    error — a rewrite of `drive()` that "corrects" the sign to the textbook
   549	    formula will strafe backward — but the flip's continued correctness under
   550	    the current wiring is unconfirmed.
   551	84. **Orphaned prototype files resolved — some removed, some kept deliberately** —
   552	    `detect_scene.py`, `llm.py`, `voice.py`, `ch340_test.py`, `thermal_benchmark.py`,
   553	    `thermal_benchmark2.py`, `quality_benchmark.py` removed from the working tree:
   554	    each is superseded by a working replacement (`detect_person.py`,
   555	    `main.parse_command`, `main.listen()`, `thermal_benchmark3.py`,
   556	    `quality_benchmark2.py`), and `ch340_test.py` is actively misleading — it
   557	    targets the wrong chip and could send someone debugging serial to a false
   558	    "board is broken" conclusion. `nav_sim.py` is kept per #23. `get_temp.py` and
   559	    `thermal_guard.py` are kept because the thermal threshold is unresolved (#75)
   560	    and these are the only per-zone readers available if it gets wired in.
   561	    `mission_test.py` is kept because it is the only harness driving `Robot` via a
   562	    `SimulatedMotors` stub — the shape #81's outstanding `run_cycle`-level test
   563	    needs. `git rm` only; history is not rewritten, so removal is reversible.
   564	
   565	
   566	## Workflow
   567	
   568	85. **Defer the #84 orphan-file deletion — the seven named files remain tracked and
   569	    present** — a direct Git audit found `detect_scene.py`, `llm.py`, `voice.py`,
   570	    `ch340_test.py`, `thermal_benchmark.py`, `thermal_benchmark2.py`, and
   571	    `quality_benchmark.py` physically present and tracked, with no staged or
   572	    unstaged deletion and no reachable deletion commit. Supersedes only #84's
   573	    removal claim: retain all seven until a separate explicit removal task is
   574	    approved. The files remain superseded/orphaned as documented in `FILES.md`.
   575	86. **Use a provider-independent Coder → independent Reviewer → human commit gate
   576	    for code changes** — Local AI and the human define the task; the Coder verifies
   577	    and automatically obtains a fresh Reviewer response; the human decides whether
   578	    to commit after Local AI discussion. A reviewer pass is not commit authority;
   579	    changed candidates require a new review and push always needs separate approval.
   580	87. **Use explicit per-task execution profiles and role-scoped Ponytail modes** —
   581	    Before phone-agent work, Local AI identifies the exact role, platform, model,
   582	    separately exposed reasoning effort, Ponytail mode, reason, and fallback.
   583	    Ponytail defaults to `lite` for ordinary coding, `full` for intentional
   584	    simplification/refactoring, `ultra` only for explicitly human-approved
   585	    disposable work, and `off` for independent review, Doc Keeper work, non-coding
   586	    work, and hardware execution. Ponytail never overrides project safety,
   587	    verification, independent-review, human commit, or push gates.
   588	
   589	## Navigation (cont. VI)
   590	
   591	88. **Resolve #81 by restoring the Python-selected safety move after the
   592	    `nav_stuck` Gemma consultation** — `run_cycle()` now saves the exact move
   593	    returned by `navigate_rules()` and restores it after Gemma processing whenever
   594	    the existing `safety_move` condition was true. Gemma remains consulted once at
   595	    the ladder's side flip; the existing safety-move set and ladder are unchanged.
   596	    Commit `d30e663` adds both-direction `run_cycle()` regression coverage with

   602	89. **A corrected `mode2_auto.ino` is committed at
